  if(m===8||m===9){c.clearRect(0,0,S,S);c.strokeStyle='rgba(70,70,70,.84)';c.fillStyle='rgba(70,70,70,.9)';c.lineWidth=12;c.beginPath();c.ellipse(256,322,150,92,-.07,0,TAU);c.stroke();c.beginPath();c.moveTo(139,259);c.lineTo(365,383);c.moveTo(150,387);c.lineTo(367,255);c.stroke();c.font='bold 180px Arial';c.textAlign='center';c.fillText(m===8?'A':'B',256,203)}
  if(m===10||m===11){c.strokeStyle='#d1d1d1';c.lineWidth=12;c.strokeRect(12,12,488,488);c.fillStyle='#dddddd';c.textAlign='center';c.font='bold 49px Arial';c.fillText(m===10?'لانگ الف':'TUNNELS B',256,140);c.font='bold 130px Arial';c.fillText(m===10?'A →':'← B',256,310);c.font='21px Arial';c.fillText('غبار ۲',256,431)}
  if(m===12){c.fillStyle='#c6c6c6';c.font='bold 67px Arial';c.textAlign='center';c.fillText('HOTEL',256,150);c.font='bold 77px Arial';c.fillText('SAHRA',256,257);c.font='38px Arial';c.fillText('✦ ✦ ✦',256,358);c.strokeStyle='#bdbdbd';c.lineWidth=5;c.strokeRect(20,20,472,472)}
  if(m===13){c.fillStyle='#333333';c.textAlign='center';c.font='bold 95px Arial';c.fillText('↑  ↑',256,168);c.font='bold 53px Arial';c.fillText('FRAGILE',256,266);c.font='33px monospace';c.fillText('D02 / 047',256,362);c.strokeStyle='#5d5d5d';c.lineWidth=6;c.strokeRect(29,29,454,454)}
  if(m===14){c.strokeStyle='#a8a8a8';c.lineWidth=9;for(let r=15;r<230;r+=30)c.strokeRect(r,r,S-2*r,S-2*r);c.lineWidth=5;for(let y=70;y<460;y+=48)for(let x=70;x<460;x+=48){c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x,y-14);c.lineTo(x+14,y);c.lineTo(x,y+14);c.lineTo(x-14,y);c.closePath();c.stroke()}}
  decorateTile(c,m,S);const tx=(m%4)*S,ty=Math.floor(m/4)*S,G=16,T=S-G*2;
  ac.drawImage(tile,tx+G,ty+G,T,T);
  ac.drawImage(tile,0,0,1,S,tx,ty+G,G,T);ac.drawImage(tile,S-1,0,1,S,tx+S-G,ty+G,G,T);
  ac.drawImage(tile,0,0,S,1,tx+G,ty,T,G);ac.drawImage(tile,0,S-1,S,1,tx+G,ty+S-G,T,G);
  for(const a of[0,1])for(const b of[0,1])ac.drawImage(tile,a*(S-1),b*(S-1),1,1,tx+a*(S-G),ty+b*(S-G),G,G);
 }
 atlasSheet=sheet;return uploadAtlas(sheet);
}
function uploadAtlas(sheet){
 const tex=gl.createTexture();gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D,tex);gl.texImage2D(gl.TEXTURE_2D,0,gl.RGBA,gl.RGBA,gl.UNSIGNED_BYTE,sheet);gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D,gl.TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER,atlasGradients?gl.LINEAR_MIPMAP_LINEAR:gl.LINEAR);gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D,gl.TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER,gl.LINEAR);gl.generateMipmap(gl.TEXTURE_2D);const ext=gl.getExtension('EXT_texture_filter_anisotropic');if(ext)gl.texParameterf(gl.TEXTURE_2D,ext.TEXTURE_MAX_ANISOTROPY_EXT,1);atlasReady=true;return tex;
}
function shader(type,src){const s=gl.createShader(type);gl.shaderSource(s,src);gl.compileShader(s);if(!gl.getShaderParameter(s,gl.COMPILE_STATUS))throw Error(gl.getShaderInfoLog(s));return s}
let atlasGradients=false,atlasPrefix='';
function textureCapabilities(){atlasGradients=!!gl.getExtension('EXT_shader_texture_lod')&&!!gl.getExtension('OES_standard_derivatives');atlasPrefix=atlasGradients?'#extension GL_EXT_shader_texture_lod : enable\n#extension GL_OES_standard_derivatives : enable\n#define ATLAS_GRADIENTS\n':'';}textureCapabilities();
const programSources=new Map();
function program(v,f){const fragmentSource=f;f=atlasPrefix+f;const p=gl.createProgram(),vs=shader(gl.VERTEX_SHADER,v),fs=shader(gl.FRAGMENT_SHADER,f);gl.attachShader(p,vs);gl.attachShader(p,fs);gl.linkProgram(p);gl.deleteShader(vs);gl.deleteShader(fs);if(!gl.getProgramParameter(p,gl.LINK_STATUS))throw Error(gl.getProgramInfoLog(p));programSources.set(p,[v,fragmentSource]);return p}
let prog=program(`
attribute vec3 p,n,c;attribute vec2 uv;attribute float material;
uniform mat4 vp,lightVP;varying vec3 col,normal,pos;varying vec2 texcoord;varying float mat;varying vec4 lightpos;
void main(){gl_Position=vp*vec4(p,1.);col=c;normal=n;pos=p;texcoord=uv;mat=material;lightpos=lightVP*vec4(p,1.);}`,`
precision highp float;
uniform mat4 lightVP;uniform sampler2D atlas,shadowMap,fabricAtlas;uniform vec3 eye;uniform float shadows,weapon;
varying vec3 col,normal,pos;varying vec2 texcoord;varying float mat;varying vec4 lightpos;
float depthAt(vec2 uv){return dot(texture2D(shadowMap,uv),vec4(1.,1./255.,1./65025.,1./16581375.));}
void main(){

 if(mat< -3.5){
  vec2 q=texcoord*2.-1.;float r=length(q),phase=col.y;
  if(mat< -5.5){float ring=exp(-pow((r-.64)*13.,2.));float core=exp(-r*r*10.);float a=(ring*.55+core*.8)*col.z;if(a<.005)discard;gl_FragColor=vec4(vec3(.96),a);return;}
  float noise=sin(q.x*13.+sin(q.y*11.+phase)*1.9+phase)*sin(q.y*15.-phase)+.45*sin(q.x*29.-q.y*23.+phase);
  if(mat< -4.5){float edge=1.-smoothstep(.40+noise*.07,.98+noise*.025,r);float grit=fract(sin(dot(floor(texcoord*64.),vec2(12.9898,78.233))+phase)*43758.5453);float cracks=pow(abs(sin(atan(q.y,q.x)*13.+sin(atan(q.y,q.x)*7.+phase))),42.)*.10*smoothstep(.3,.8,r);float a=edge*(.58+.28*(1.-r)+noise*.08-cracks)*col.z;if(a<.015)discard;gl_FragColor=vec4(vec3(col.x+noise*.018+grit*.025),a);return;}
  float edge=1.-smoothstep(.23+noise*.05,1.,r);float a=edge*col.z;if(a<.005)discard;gl_FragColor=vec4(vec3(col.x+noise*.033+(1.-r)*.065),a);return;
 }
 vec3 n=normalize(normal),L=normalize(vec3(-.48,.78,-.40));vec4 tex=vec4(1.);
 if(mat>15.5){float m=floor(mat+.1)-16.;vec2 tile=vec2(mod(m,2.),floor(m/2.));vec2 at=(tile+.03125+fract(texcoord)*.9375)/2.;
 #ifdef ATLAS_GRADIENTS
 tex=texture2DGradEXT(fabricAtlas,at,dFdx(texcoord)*.46875,dFdy(texcoord)*.46875);
 #else
 tex=texture2D(fabricAtlas,at);
 #endif
 }else if(mat>-.5){float m=floor(mat+.1);vec2 tile=vec2(mod(m,4.),floor(m/4.));vec2 at=(tile+.03125+fract(texcoord)*.9375)/4.;
 #ifdef ATLAS_GRADIENTS
 vec2 gx=dFdx(texcoord)*.234375,gy=dFdy(texcoord)*.234375;
 tex=texture2DGradEXT(atlas,at,gx,gy);
 #else
 tex=texture2D(atlas,at);
 #endif
 if(tex.a<.3)discard;}
 float diffuse=max(dot(n,L),0.),sh=1.;vec3 lp=lightpos.xyz/lightpos.w*.5+.5;
 if(shadows>.5&&weapon<.5&&lp.x>0.&&lp.x<1.&&lp.y>0.&&lp.y<1.&&lp.z<1.){
  float bias=.00035+.00025*(1.-diffuse);sh=0.;vec3 plane=(lightVP*vec4(n,0.)).xyz*vec3(5625.,5625.,6320.25);vec2 slope=abs(plane.z)>.0001?clamp(-plane.xy/plane.z,vec2(-6.),vec2(6.)):vec2(0.);
  for(int x=-1;x<=1;x++)for(int y=-1;y<=1;y++){vec2 offset=vec2(float(x),float(y))*1.2/2048.;vec2 sampleUV=(floor((lp.xy+offset)*2048.)+.5)/2048.;sh+=lp.z+dot(slope,sampleUV-lp.xy)-bias<=depthAt(sampleUV)?1.:.24;}
  sh/=9.;
 }
 vec3 light=vec3(.49,.51,.50)+vec3(.74,.68,.58)*diffuse*sh+vec3(.11,.12,.12)*max(n.y,0.);
 if(pos.x>3.4&&pos.x<30.&&pos.z>34.&&pos.z<57.&&weapon<.5){light+=vec3(.20,.11,.025);}
 if(weapon>.5)light=vec3(.57)+vec3(.58,.52,.42)*diffuse;
 vec3 result=col*tex.rgb*light;
 if(mat>4.5&&mat<5.5){vec3 V=normalize(eye-pos);result+=vec3(.15)*pow(max(dot(reflect(-L,n),V),0.),28.)*sh;}
 if(mat< -1.5)result=col;
 float fog=weapon>.5?0.:1.-exp(-pow(length(pos-eye)*.008,2.));
 result=mix(result,vec3(.76,.79,.75),clamp(fog,0.,.84));if(mat> -2.5){float ink=dot(result,vec3(.2126,.7152,.0722));result=vec3(clamp((ink-.38)*1.12+.38,0.,1.));}gl_FragColor=vec4(pow(max(result,vec3(0.)),vec3(.94)),1.);
}`);
let shadowProg=program(`attribute vec3 p;uniform mat4 lightVP;void main(){gl_Position=lightVP*vec4(p,1.);}`,`
precision highp float;void main(){vec4 e=fract(gl_FragCoord.z*vec4(1.,255.,65025.,16581375.));e-=e.yzww*vec4(1./255.,1./255.,1./255.,0.);gl_FragColor=e;}`);
let skyProg=program(`attribute vec2 p;varying vec2 q;void main(){q=p;gl_Position=vec4(p,.999,1.);}`,`
precision mediump float;varying vec2 q;uniform vec3 forward,right,up;uniform float aspect,tanFov;
void main(){vec3 ray=normalize(forward+right*q.x*aspect*tanFov+up*q.y*tanFov);float h=clamp(ray.y*.8+.12,0.,1.);vec3 col=mix(vec3(.83,.83,.73),vec3(.30,.54,.69),pow(h,.55));float sun=pow(max(dot(ray,normalize(vec3(-.48,.78,-.40))),0.),200.);col+=vec3(.30,.24,.12)*sun;float haze=sin(ray.x*7.+ray.z*3.)*sin(ray.z*11.-ray.x*2.);col+=max(haze,0.)*.035*smoothstep(.05,.6,ray.y);float gray=dot(col,vec3(.2126,.7152,.0722));gl_FragColor=vec4(vec3(gray*.70+.28),1.);}`);
const attrs={};for(const name of['p','n','c','uv','material'])attrs[name]=gl.getAttribLocation(prog,name);
const uniforms={};for(const name of['vp','lightVP','eye','atlas','shadowMap','fabricAtlas','shadows','weapon'])uniforms[name]=gl.getUniformLocation(prog,name);
boot.stage(32,'آماده‌سازی بافت‌های نقشه…');
let atlas=makeAtlas();fabricTexture=makeFabricAtlas();
// Architectural surfaces retain the traced plan. Vaults and gateways share their height profiles with collision.
function faceStroke(a,b,r,col,angle){
 const n=[Math.sin(angle),0,Math.cos(angle)],v=norm(cross(sub(b,a),n)).map(x=>x*r);
 const q=(p,s)=>p.map((x,i)=>x+s*v[i]);quad(stat,q(a,-1),q(b,-1),q(b,1),q(a,1),col);
}
// Street-facing Kashan houses: earthen plaster, brick portals, small shuttered openings.
function archCurve(u,w,h){
 const t=clamp(Math.abs(u)/(w/2),0,1),rise=Math.min(w*.48,h*.38);
 return h-rise+rise*Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,2.25-(t+.5)*(t+.5)))/Math.sqrt(2);
}
// Raised gereh straps, connected octagonal knots and lozenges, integrated with masonry frames.
function gerehPanel(x,y,z,w,h,angle,depth=.12,back=true){
 const p=(u,v,d=depth)=>facePoint(x,z,angle,u,y+v,d);
 if(back)box(stat,...p(0,0,depth-.025),w,h,.035,0x6f6c63,angle,0);
 const weight=.012,margin=weight/2;
 // Liang–Barsky clipping: the motif never gets squeezed to fit a narrow frieze.
 function strap(a,b,col=0xe3dac5){
  let lo=0,hi=1;const dx=b[0]-a[0],dy=b[1]-a[1];
  for(const [v,q]of[[-dx,a[0]+w/2-margin],[dx,w/2-margin-a[0]],[-dy,a[1]+h/2-margin],[dy,h/2-margin-a[1]]]){
   if(Math.abs(v)<1e-9){if(q<0)return}else if(v<0)lo=Math.max(lo,q/v);else hi=Math.min(hi,q/v);
  }
  if(lo>=hi)return;const A=[a[0]+dx*lo,a[1]+dy*lo],B=[a[0]+dx*hi,a[1]+dy*hi],len=Math.hypot(dx,dy);if(len<.001)return;
  const nx=-dy/len*weight/2,ny=dx/len*weight/2;
  quad(stat,p(A[0]+nx,A[1]+ny),p(A[0]-nx,A[1]-ny),p(B[0]-nx,B[1]-ny),p(B[0]+nx,B[1]+ny),col,7);
 }
 const cell=GEREH_MODULE,r=cell*.44;
 for(let j=-Math.ceil(h/cell/2);j<=Math.ceil(h/cell/2);j++)for(let i=-Math.ceil(w/cell/2);i<=Math.ceil(w/cell/2);i++){
  const cx=i*cell,cy=j*cell,pts=[];
  for(let k=0;k<16;k++){const a=k*Math.PI/8,rr=k%2?r*.765:r;pts.push([cx+Math.cos(a)*rr,cy+Math.sin(a)*rr])}
  for(let k=0;k<16;k++)strap(pts[k],pts[(k+1)%16]);
  for(const d of[-1,1])for(const e of[-1,1]){
   strap([cx+d*r,cy],[cx+d*cell/2,cy+e*cell*.25]);
   strap([cx,cy+d*r],[cx+e*cell*.22,cy+d*cell/2]);
  }
 }
}

function ornamentPortal(g){
 if(g.flatGate)return;
 const {x,z,angle,width,height,depth}=g,base=groundAt(x,z),pt=(u,y,d)=>facePoint(x,z,angle,u,base+y,d);
 for(const side of[-1,1]){
  const d=side*(depth/2+.04);
  for(const end of[-1,1])box(stat,...pt(end*(width/2+.17),height*.5,d),.22,height-.15,.075,0xcbbda1,angle,1);
  box(stat,...pt(0,height-.19,d),width+.48,.21,.07,0xb9ad93,angle,1);
  for(let u=-width/2;u<width/2;u+=.25)box(stat,...pt(u,height-.34,d+side*.02),.13,.08,.09,0xcec0a4,angle,7);
  const facing=side>0?angle:angle+Math.PI;
  for(const end of[-1,1]){
   const u=end*width*.37,center=pt(u,height-.95,side*(depth/2+.08));
   const clear=gatewayRise(g,Math.max(0,Math.abs(u)-width*.09));
   
  }
  const center=pt(0,height-.20,side*(depth/2+.12));
  gerehPanel(center[0],center[1],center[2],width*.90,.23,facing,.02,false);
 }
}
function kashanRoofline(){
 for(const [px,pz,r]of[[1030,545,3.0],[480,479,2.1],[746,1080,2.5]]){
  const p=wp(px,pz),ring=MAP_LOOPS.findIndex((loop,i)=>i>0&&polygonContains(loop,p.x,p.z));if(ring<1)continue;
  const h=Math.max(...MAP_LOOPS[ring].map(q=>groundAt(...q)))+6.8+(ring%3)*.65,start=stat.length;
  dome(stat,p.x,h,p.z,r,0xc5baa2,6);
  cylinder(stat,p.x,h+r*.62+.05,p.z,.22,.13,0xb7aa8e,1,12);
  // Rectangular windcatcher, behind the street wall, within the existing closed building island.
  const bx=p.x+r*.70,bz=p.z+r*.50,bw=1.8,bh=3.35;
  if(![[-1,-1],[-1,1],[1,-1],[1,1]].every(([a,b])=>polygonContains(MAP_LOOPS[ring],bx+a*bw*.6,bz+b*bw*.6)))continue;
  box(stat,bx,h+bh/2,bz,bw,bh,bw,0xd1c5ac,0,0);
  for(let side=0;side<4;side++){
   const angle=side*Math.PI/2,pt=(u,y,d)=>facePoint(bx,bz,angle,u,h+y,d);
   for(let k=-1;k<=1;k++){
    box(stat,...pt(k*.46,2.02,bw/2+.018),.25,1.93,.035,0x42413a,angle);
    box(stat,...pt(k*.46,1.91,bw/2+.09),.045,1.90,.15,0xc7baa0,angle,1);
   }
  }
  box(stat,bx,h+bh+.04,bz,bw+.24,.15,bw+.24,0xc5b79a,0,1);
  box(stat,bx,h+1.0,bz,bw+.12,.14,bw+.12,0xbeb096,0,1);
 }
}

function brickNoise(n){const f=Math.sin(n*127.1+311.7)*43758.5453;return f-Math.floor(f)}
function oldBrickCourses(c,m,S){
 c.fillStyle='#a59c8a';c.fillRect(0,0,S,S);
 const bw=64,bh=25.6;
 for(let row=-1;row<=20;row++)for(let col=-1;col<=8;col++){
  const id=((row+20)%20)*19+((col+8)%8)*71+m*31,noise=k=>brickNoise(id+k*17),x=col*bw+(row%2)*bw/2,y=row*bh,j=.7+noise(0)*.9;
  const points=[[x+j+noise(1)*1.4,y+1.3],[x+bw-2-noise(2),y+.8+noise(3)],[x+bw-1.2,y+bh*.42],[x+bw-1.8-noise(4),y+bh-1.5],[x+bw*.38,y+bh-1-noise(5)],[x+1.3,y+bh-2.0],[x+noise(6),y+bh*.50]];
  const value=Math.round(153+noise(7)*39);c.fillStyle=`rgb(${value},${value-5},${value-16})`;c.beginPath();points.forEach((p,i)=>i?c.lineTo(...p):c.moveTo(...p));c.closePath();c.fill();
  c.save();c.clip();const light=c.createLinearGradient(x,y,x,y+bh);light.addColorStop(0,'rgba(247,237,207,.16)');light.addColorStop(.35,'rgba(0,0,0,0)');light.addColorStop(1,'rgba(44,36,27,.18)');c.fillStyle=light;c.fillRect(x,y,bw,bh);
  for(let k=0;k<15;k++){const px=x+noise(k+8)*bw,py=y+noise(k+33)*bh;c.fillStyle=k%3?'rgba(61,47,32,.09)':'rgba(243,224,187,.17)';c.beginPath();c.ellipse(px,py,.5+noise(k+60)*2,.5+noise(k+76),noise(k+91)*3,0,TAU);c.fill()}
  if(noise(122)>.64){c.strokeStyle='rgba(71,55,39,.20)';c.lineWidth=.6;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x+noise(124)*bw,y);c.lineTo(x+noise(124)*bw+3,y+bh*.42);c.lineTo(x+noise(124)*bw+1,y+bh*.68);c.stroke()}
  c.restore();
 }
}
function oldBrickFace(c,S){
 // Soft chipped edges and firing marks are baked into the existing tile. No
 // bevel modifiers, extra meshes or per-frame random noise are needed.
 for(let i=0;i<100;i++){
  const side=i%4,k=brickNoise(i*23),a=k*S,r=1+brickNoise(i*47)*5,x=side===0?0:side===1?S: a,y=side===2?0:side===3?S:a;
  c.fillStyle=i%3?'rgba(70,59,43,.22)':'rgba(235,221,188,.30)';c.beginPath();c.ellipse(x,y,r*1.6,r,brickNoise(i*71)*TAU,0,TAU);c.fill();
 }
 for(let k=0;k<25;k++){c.strokeStyle='rgba(66,53,35,.11)';c.lineWidth=.6+brickNoise(k*7);const x=brickNoise(k*19)*S,y=brickNoise(k*31)*S;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x,y);c.lineTo(x+3,y+10+brickNoise(k*43)*18);c.lineTo(x+1,y+18+brickNoise(k*43)*18);c.stroke()}
}

function kashanMaterials(c,m,S){
 const tone=v=>`rgb(${v},${Math.round(v*.98)},${Math.round(v*.92)})`;
 if(m===0){
  // Straw-bearing earthen plaster, without large outlined blocks or decorative stamps.
  c.fillStyle='#c9c3b6';c.fillRect(0,0,S,S);
  for(let j=0;j<3100;j++){const x=rand()*S,y=rand()*S,r=rand()*13+1;c.fillStyle=j%2?'rgba(72,65,52,.018)':'rgba(255,251,234,.03)';c.beginPath();c.ellipse(x,y,r,r*.6,rand()*3,0,TAU);c.fill()}
  for(let j=0;j<1600;j++){const x=rand()*S,y=rand()*S;c.strokeStyle=j%3?'rgba(92,80,59,.10)':'rgba(243,237,212,.20)';c.lineWidth=.6;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x,y);c.lineTo(x+rand()*5-2,y+rand()*6-3);c.stroke()}
  c.strokeStyle='rgba(89,79,59,.10)';c.lineWidth=.5;for(let j=0;j<6;j++){let x=rand()*S,y=rand()*S;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x,y);for(let k=0;k<6;k++){x+=rand()*9-4;y+=rand()*9;c.lineTo(x,y)}c.stroke()}
 }
 if(m===1||m===6){
  c.fillStyle='#8d887c';c.fillRect(0,0,S,S);const bw=m===6?64:64,bh=m===6?24:24;
  for(let row=-1;row<=S/bh;row++)for(let col=-1;col<=S/bw;col++){
   const x=col*bw+(row%2)*bw/2,y=row*bh,v=158+rand()*39;
   c.fillStyle=tone(v);c.fillRect(x+1.3,y+1.2,bw-2.6,bh-2.4);
   c.fillStyle='rgba(255,245,220,.15)';c.fillRect(x+2,y+2,bw-4,1);
   c.fillStyle='rgba(43,37,26,.12)';c.fillRect(x+2,y+bh-2,bw-4,1);
   for(let k=0;k<7;k++){c.fillStyle=k%2?'rgba(52,44,29,.09)':'rgba(249,237,210,.11)';c.fillRect(x+3+rand()*(bw-6),y+3+rand()*(bh-6),1+rand()*5,1+rand()*2)}
  }
 }
 if(m===2||m===15){
  c.fillStyle='#858278';c.fillRect(0,0,S,S);
  const rows=[0,85,170,256,341,426,512];
  for(let row=0;row<rows.length-1;row++){
   const hh=rows[row+1]-rows[row];let x=-((row%3)*47);
   while(x<S){const w=92+Math.round(rand()*38),y=rows[row],v=164+Math.round(rand()*32);c.fillStyle=tone(v);c.fillRect(x+1.4,y+1.4,w-2.8,hh-2.8);
    c.strokeStyle='rgba(239,234,220,.32)';c.lineWidth=1;c.strokeRect(x+3,y+3,w-6,hh-6);
    for(let k=0;k<45;k++){c.fillStyle=k%2?'rgba(30,28,24,.025)':'rgba(255,250,231,.05)';c.beginPath();c.ellipse(x+rand()*w,y+rand()*hh,rand()*7+1,rand()*4+1,0,0,TAU);c.fill()}
    x+=w;
   }
  }
 }
 if(m===3){c.fillStyle='#77766e';c.fillRect(0,0,S,S);for(let j=0;j<14000;j++){const v=92+rand()*57;c.fillStyle=tone(v);c.fillRect(rand()*S,rand()*S,1.2,1.2)}}
 if(m===7){c.fillStyle='#c8c0ae';c.fillRect(0,0,S,S);for(let j=0;j<2600;j++){c.fillStyle=j%2?'rgba(53,47,35,.035)':'rgba(255,246,221,.05)';c.fillRect(rand()*S,rand()*S,rand()*5+1,rand()*3+1)}}
}

function centeredInk(c,text,x,y){
 c.save();c.direction='ltr';c.textAlign='center';c.textBaseline='alphabetic';
 const m=c.measureText(text),dx=(m.actualBoundingBoxLeft-m.actualBoundingBoxRight)/2,dy=(m.actualBoundingBoxAscent-m.actualBoundingBoxDescent)/2;
 c.fillText(text,x+dx,y+dy);c.restore();
}
function decorateTile(c,m,S){
 const star=(x,y,r,points=8)=>{c.beginPath();for(let i=0;i<points*2;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/points,rr=i%2?r*.47:r;c.lineTo(x+Math.cos(a)*rr,y+Math.sin(a)*rr)}c.closePath();c.stroke()};
 kashanMaterials(c,m,S);agedSurfaces(c,m,S);if(m===1||m===6)oldBrickCourses(c,m,S);if(m===15)oldBrickFace(c,S);
 // Limestone stays quiet so the carved geometry supplies the detail.

 if([8,9,10,11,12,13].includes(m)){
  c.clearRect(0,0,S,S);if(m!==8&&m!==9){c.fillStyle='#252525';c.fillRect(0,0,S,S);c.strokeStyle='#ddd';c.lineWidth=8;c.strokeRect(16,16,480,480);c.lineWidth=2;c.strokeRect(28,28,456,456)}
  c.direction='rtl';c.textAlign='center';c.fillStyle=m===8||m===9?'#292929':'#f4f4f4';
  c.font=(m===12?'158':'96')+'px Nastaliq';
  const text=({8:'الف',9:'ب',10:'درب الف',11:'تونل ب',12:'غبار ۲',13:'غبار ۲'})[m];
  centeredInk(c,text,256,m===8||m===9?310:256);
  if(m===8||m===9){c.strokeStyle='#393939';c.lineWidth=7;c.beginPath();c.ellipse(256,310,166,80,0,0,TAU);c.stroke()}

 }
 if(m===13)persianChestTile(c,S);if(m===3)gerehRepeatTile(c,S);
}

function gerehRepeatTile(c,S){
 c.clearRect(0,0,S,S);c.fillStyle='#655b4d';c.fillRect(0,0,S,S);
 const cell=S/2,r=cell*.44;
 const line=(a,b)=>{c.beginPath();c.moveTo(...a);c.lineTo(...b);c.stroke()};
 c.strokeStyle='#b5a88a';c.lineWidth=S*.012/SURFACE_METERS[3];
 // Identical 600 mm knots to the raised wall gereh; two modules per atlas repeat.
 for(let j=-1;j<=2;j++)for(let i=-1;i<=2;i++){
  const x=(i+.5)*cell,y=(j+.5)*cell,pts=[];
  for(let k=0;k<16;k++){const a=k*Math.PI/8,rr=k%2?r*.765:r;pts.push([x+Math.cos(a)*rr,y+Math.sin(a)*rr])}
  for(let k=0;k<16;k++)line(pts[k],pts[(k+1)%16]);
  for(const d of[-1,1])for(const e of[-1,1]){line([x+d*r,y],[x+d*cell/2,y+e*cell*.25]);line([x,y+d*r],[x+e*cell*.22,y+d*cell/2])}
 }
}
function persianChestTile(c,S){
 c.clearRect(0,0,S,S);c.fillStyle='#655b4d';c.fillRect(0,0,S,S);
 // Aged timber ground, khatam corner bands and interlaced eight-fold girih.
 for(let k=0;k<1100;k++){const x=rand()*S,y=rand()*470;c.fillStyle=k%2?'rgba(231,219,186,.06)':'rgba(20,19,16,.08)';c.fillRect(x,y,.7+rand()*1.7,5+rand()*27)}
 for(let k=0;k<8;k++){c.strokeStyle=k%2?'#b6a789':'#3e3a31';c.lineWidth=2;c.strokeRect(8+k*3,8+k*3,496-k*6,454-k*6)}
 const line=(pts,col,width=2)=>{c.strokeStyle=col;c.lineWidth=width;c.beginPath();pts.forEach(([x,y],i)=>i?c.lineTo(x,y):c.moveTo(x,y));c.stroke()};
 for(let row=0;row<4;row++)for(let col=0;col<5;col++){
  const x=66+col*94,y=66+row*102,pts=[];
  for(let k=0;k<=16;k++){const a=k*Math.PI/8,r=k%2?20:36;pts.push([x+Math.cos(a)*r,y+Math.sin(a)*r])}
  line(pts,'#b5a88a',2.8);
  line([[x-33,y-33],[x+33,y-33],[x+33,y+33],[x-33,y+33],[x-33,y-33]],'#8e8068',1.7);
 }
 for(let x=30;x<482;x+=15)for(const y of[22,448]){c.fillStyle=(x/15|0)%2?'#cec0a0':'#3f3b32';c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x,y-5);c.lineTo(x+6,y);c.lineTo(x,y+5);c.lineTo(x-6,y);c.closePath();c.fill()}
 c.fillStyle='#494337';c.fillRect(106,166,300,142);c.strokeStyle='#c3b293';c.lineWidth=2;c.strokeRect(109,169,294,136);
 c.direction='rtl';c.textAlign='center';c.fillStyle='#d9cbb0';c.font='86px Nastaliq';centeredInk(c,'غبار ۲',256,237);
 // Small dedicated plate strip, sampled only by the Peykan model.
 c.fillStyle='#dadbd2';c.fillRect(1,478,190,33);c.fillStyle='#292b27';c.font='bold 23px Arial';c.fillText('۲۱  ۳۴۵  ۶۷',95,503);
}

// Ground follows the traced plan. Extra ground underneath closed buildings is never navigable.
for(let z=0;z<SIZE;z++)for(let x=0;x<SIZE;x++){
 const material=2;
 const floor=(px,pz)=>floorCell(x,z,px,pz);
 quad(stat,[x,floor(x,z+1),z+1],[x+1,floor(x+1,z+1),z+1],[x+1,floor(x+1,z),z],[x,floor(x,z),z],0xffffff,material,[[x/3,(z+1)/3],[(x+1)/3,(z+1)/3],[(x+1)/3,z/3],[x/3,z/3]]);
 
}
drawRetainingEdges();
box(stat,48,-3,48,180,.4,180,0xc5b995,0,2);
function facePoint(x,z,angle,u,v,depth=0){return[x+Math.cos(angle)*u+Math.sin(angle)*depth,v,z-Math.sin(angle)*u+Math.cos(angle)*depth]}
function plaque(x,y,z,w,h,angle,mat){SIGN_REQUESTS.push({x,y,z,w,h,angle,mat})}

function roofPolygon(poly,y){
 const pts=poly.slice();let area=0;for(let i=0;i<pts.length;i++){const a=pts[i],b=pts[(i+1)%pts.length];area+=a[0]*b[1]-b[0]*a[1]}if(area<0)pts.reverse();
 const cross=(a,b,c)=>(b[0]-a[0])*(c[1]-a[1])-(b[1]-a[1])*(c[0]-a[0]);let guard=0;
 while(pts.length>=3&&guard++<poly.length*poly.length){let cut=false;for(let i=0;i<pts.length;i++){
  const a=pts[(i+pts.length-1)%pts.length],b=pts[i],c=pts[(i+1)%pts.length];if(cross(a,b,c)<=.00001)continue;
  if(pts.some(p=>p!==a&&p!==b&&p!==c&&cross(a,b,p)>=0&&cross(b,c,p)>=0&&cross(c,a,p)>=0))continue;
  quad(stat,[a[0],y,a[1]],[c[0],y,c[1]],[b[0],y,b[1]],[a[0],y,a[1]],0xffffff,2,[[a[0]/3,a[1]/3],[c[0]/3,c[1]/3],[b[0]/3,b[1]/3],[a[0]/3,a[1]/3]]);pts.splice(i,1);cut=true;break;
 }if(!cut)break;}
}
const vaultArcCache=new Map();
function vaultArcLength(half,offset){
 if(half<.01)return 0;const key=half.toFixed(5);let table=vaultArcCache.get(key);
 if(!table){table=[0];const rise=Math.min(3.3,half*1.10),height=t=>rise*Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,2.25-(t+.5)**2))/Math.sqrt(2);let y=height(0);
  for(let i=1;i<=128;i++){const next=height(i/128);table.push(table[i-1]+Math.hypot(half/128,next-y));y=next}vaultArcCache.set(key,table)}
 const u=clamp(Math.abs(offset)/half,0,1)*128,i=Math.min(127,Math.floor(u));return Math.sign(offset)*(table[i]+(table[i+1]-table[i])*(u-i));
}
function vaultUV(r,p,axisOverride=null){
 let axis=axisOverride||([1,5].includes(r.roofIndex)?'x':'z');
 if(!axisOverride&&r.roofIndex===0)axis=p[2]>=618*MAP_SCALE&&p[2]<=750*MAP_SCALE?'x':'z';
 if(!axisOverride&&r.roofIndex===2)axis=p[2]>=335*MAP_SCALE?'x':'z';
 const q=vaultSection(r,p[0],p[2],axis);let lo=q.lo,hi=q.hi;
 if(r.roofIndex===0&&axis==='x'){lo=Math.max(lo,618*MAP_SCALE);hi=Math.min(hi,750*MAP_SCALE)}
 if(r.roofIndex===2){lo=(axis==='x'?335:770)*MAP_SCALE;hi=(axis==='x'?414:830)*MAP_SCALE}
 const half=(hi-lo)/2,offset=q.across-(lo+hi)/2,arc=vaultArcLength(half,offset);
 return axis==='x'?[q.along/SURFACE_METERS[6],arc/SURFACE_METERS[6]]:[arc/SURFACE_METERS[6],q.along/SURFACE_METERS[6]];
}
function buildVaults(){
 const thick=.31;
 for(const r of ROOFS){
  const [x0,z0,x1,z1]=r.bounds;
  for(let ix=0;ix<Math.ceil((x1-x0)/vaultStep(r));ix++)for(let iz=0;iz<Math.ceil((z1-z0)/vaultStep(r));iz++){
   const cell=vaultCell(r,ix,iz);if(!cell)continue;
   const top=v=>[v[0],v[1]+thick,v[2]];
   for(const [a,b,c]of cell.triangles){
    let axis=null;
    if(r.roofIndex===0){const z=(a[2]+b[2]+c[2])/3;axis=z>=618*MAP_SCALE&&z<=750*MAP_SCALE?'x':'z'}
    if(r.roofIndex===2){const x=(a[0]+b[0]+c[0])/3,z=(a[2]+b[2]+c[2])/3,dx=Math.abs(rawVaultHeightAt(r,x+.01,z)-rawVaultHeightAt(r,x-.01,z)),dz=Math.abs(rawVaultHeightAt(r,x,z+.01)-rawVaultHeightAt(r,x,z-.01));axis=dx>dz?'z':'x'}
    const uv=v=>vaultUV(r,v,axis);
    surfaceTriangle(stat,a,b,c,0xd4c5ab,6,[uv(a),uv(b),uv(c)]);
    const aa=top(a),bb=top(b),cc=top(c);surfaceTriangle(stat,aa,cc,bb,0xbdb3a0,1,[uv(aa),uv(cc),uv(bb)]);
   }
  }
  // A bonded brick fascia replaces the unsupported white edging.
  for(let i=0;i<r.polygon.length;i++){
   const a=r.polygon[i],b=r.polygon[(i+1)%r.polygon.length],len=Math.hypot(b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1]),n=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(len/.24));
   const v=t=>{const x=a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*t,z=a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*t;return[x,vaultHeightAt(r,x,z),z]};
   for(let j=0;j<n;j++){const A=v(j/n),B=v((j+1)/n);quad(stat,A,B,[B[0],B[1]+thick,B[2]],[A[0],A[1]+thick,A[2]],0xb3a88e,15)}
  }
 }
 for(const frame of VAULT_FRAMES){if(frame.r.roofIndex===2&&((frame.axis==='x'&&frame.along>51.7&&!frame.mouth)||(frame.axis==='z'&&frame.along>22.1)))continue;vaultBrickFrame(frame);}
}
function vaultBrickFrame(f){
 const {r,axis,along,lo,hi,mouth,inward}=f,n=Math.max(14,Math.ceil((hi-lo)/.17)),pts=[];
 // At the mouth the crown retreats into the passage; the springing stays on its pier.
 const at=(u,y,d)=>axis==='z'?[u,y,along+d]:[along+d,y,u];
 for(let j=0;j<=n;j++){
  const u=lo+(hi-lo)*j/n,x=axis==='z'?u:along,z=axis==='z'?along:u;
  pts.push([u,vaultHeightAt(r,x,z)]);
 }
 for(let j=0;j<n;j++){
  const a=pts[j],b=pts[j+1],pa=pts[Math.max(0,j-1)],pb=pts[Math.min(n,j+2)];
  const normal=(a,b)=>{const dx=b[0]-a[0],dy=b[1]-a[1],d=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;return[-dy/d,dx/d]},na=normal(pa,b),nb=normal(a,pb);
  const gap=.025,A=[a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*gap,a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*gap],B=[b[0]+(a[0]-b[0])*gap,b[1]+(a[1]-b[1])*gap],D=[A[0]+na[0]*.26,A[1]+na[1]*.26],C=[B[0]+nb[0]*.26,B[1]+nb[1]*.26];
  const recess=p=>mouth?inward*.24*clamp((p[1]-r.bearing)/2.6,0,1):0;
  const poly=[A,B,C,D].map((p,k)=>[p[0]+(brickNoise(j*53+k)-.5)*.008,p[1]+(brickNoise(j*29+k)-.5)*.006]),front=poly.map(p=>at(...p,recess(p)-.19)),back=poly.map(p=>at(...p,recess(p)+.19)),col=[0xc4b69b,0xb8a98e,0xd1c2a8,0xbdb199][j%4];
  quad(stat,front[0],front[1],front[2],front[3],col,15);
  quad(stat,back[3],back[2],back[1],back[0],col,15);
  for(const k of[0,2])quad(stat,front[k],back[k],back[(k+1)%4],front[(k+1)%4],col,15);
 }
}
function vaultPier(b){
 const base=b.foundation,top=b.top,side=b.axis==='z'?'x':'z';
 box(stat,b.x,(base+top)/2,b.z,b.w,top-base,b.d,0xc2b49a,0,1);
 box(stat,b.x,groundAt(b.x,b.z)+.12,b.z,b.w+.055,.24,b.d+.055,0xaaa18e,0,7);
 box(stat,b.x,top-.05,b.z,b.w+.075,.16,b.d+.075,0xc8bda6,0,1);
 // A continuous skewback actually meets the arch foot, rather than a free edge.
 box(stat,b.x,top+.065,b.z,b.w+.04,.10,b.d+.045,0xb5a88f,0,15);
}
function bridgeProp(b){
 const floor=b.fixedBottom??groundAt(b.x,b.z),top=b.top;
 if(b.kind==='bridgeInfill'){
  // South face is recessed to the adjoining house line, with white lime plaster.
  const x0=b.x-b.w/2,x1=b.x+b.w/2,z0=b.z-b.d/2,z1=b.z+b.d/2;
  const face=(A,B)=>quad(stat,[A[0],floor,A[1]],[B[0],floor,B[1]],[B[0],top,B[1]],[A[0],top,A[1]],0xf6f3eb,0,[[0,-floor/3],[Math.hypot(B[0]-A[0],B[1]-A[1])/3,-floor/3],[Math.hypot(B[0]-A[0],B[1]-A[1])/3,-top/3],[0,-top/3]]);
  face([x0,z0],[x1,z0]);face([x1,z1],[x0,z1]);face([x0,z1],[x0,z0]);face([x1,z0],[x1,z1]);
  quad(stat,[x0,top,z0],[x0,top,z1],[x1,top,z1],[x1,top,z0],0xf6f3eb,0);
  // Flush plinth follows the pavement, without introducing a protruding pier.
  for(let x=x0;x<x1;x+=.5){const ex=Math.min(x+.5,x1),ya=groundAt(x,z0)+.24,yb=groundAt(ex,z0)+.24;quad(stat,[x,floor,z0-.003],[ex,floor,z0-.003],[ex,yb,z0-.003],[x,ya,z0-.003],0xc4bbab,7)}
 }else if(b.kind==='bridgeApron'||b.kind==='bridgeAbutment'){
  box(stat,b.x,(floor+top)/2,b.z,b.w,top-floor,b.d,0xbeb29a,0,1,16);
  const corners=[[b.x-b.w/2,top,b.z+b.d/2],[b.x+b.w/2,top,b.z+b.d/2],[b.x+b.w/2,top,b.z-b.d/2],[b.x-b.w/2,top,b.z-b.d/2]];quad(stat,...corners,0xffffff,2,corners.map(q=>[q[0]/3,q[2]/3]));
 }else if(b.kind==='bridgeSideWall'){
  box(stat,b.x,(floor+top)/2,b.z,b.w,top-floor,b.d,0xc7b99d,0,1);
  box(stat,b.x,top+.035,b.z,b.w+.10,.07,b.d+.02,0xd6c7ad,0,7);
 }else if(b.kind==='bridgeDeck'){
  box(stat,b.x,(floor+top)/2,b.z,b.w,top-floor,b.d,0xbeb29a,0,1,16);
  quad(stat,[b.x-b.w/2,top,b.z+b.d/2],[b.x+b.w/2,top,b.z+b.d/2],[b.x+b.w/2,top,b.z-b.d/2],[b.x-b.w/2,top,b.z-b.d/2],0xffffff,2,[[ (b.x-b.w/2)/3,(b.z+b.d/2)/3],[(b.x+b.w/2)/3,(b.z+b.d/2)/3],[(b.x+b.w/2)/3,(b.z-b.d/2)/3],[(b.x-b.w/2)/3,(b.z-b.d/2)/3]]);
  for(const side of[-1,1])box(stat,b.x+side*(b.w/2-.055),top-.095,b.z,.11,.07,b.d-.024,0xc8bca2,0,7);
  // A bonded spring course and tapered corbels tie the deck into its two walls.
  for(const z of[23.30,28.78])for(const side of[-1,1]){
   const x=b.x+side*(b.w/2-.16);box(stat,x,3.58,z,.31,.28,.30,0xb5a68a,0,1);
   quad(stat,[x-.15,3.45,z-.15],[x+.15,3.45,z-.15],[x+.15,3.72,z+.15],[x-.15,3.72,z+.15],0xc8b799,15);
  }
 }else if(b.kind==='bridgeStep'){
  const bottom=Math.min(top-.15,Math.max(groundAt(b.x,b.z)-.06,floor));box(stat,b.x,(bottom+top-.054)/2,b.z,b.w,top-bottom-.054,b.d,0xb7ab92,0,1,16);
  box(stat,b.x,top-.027,b.z,b.w+.018,.054,b.d+.018,0xd4c7ad,0,7,16);
  const corners=[[b.x-b.w/2,top,b.z+b.d/2],[b.x+b.w/2,top,b.z+b.d/2],[b.x+b.w/2,top,b.z-b.d/2],[b.x-b.w/2,top,b.z-b.d/2]];quad(stat,...corners,0xffffff,2,corners.map(q=>[q[0]/3,q[2]/3]));
 }else box(stat,b.x,(floor+top)/2,b.z,b.w,top-floor,b.d,0x666761,0,5);
}
function bridgeWall(b){
 const w=WALK_BRIDGE.doorW,h=WALK_BRIDGE.doorH,profile=archPoints(w,h),floor=WALK_BRIDGE.deck;
 for(const side of[-1,1]){
  const angle=side>0?0:Math.PI,z=b.z+side*b.d/2,x=b.x,seg={x,z,nx:0,nz:side,angle,length:b.w,top:b.top,tint:0xcfbea0,material:1,pt:(u,y,d=0)=>facePoint(x,z,angle,u,y,d)},u=(WALK_BRIDGE.x-b.x)*side;
  const o={type:'passage',u,y:floor,w,h,profile,arched:true,depth:-b.d};seg.holes=[o];masonryFace(seg,[o]);FACADE_SURFACES.push(seg);
  const pt=(a,y,d)=>seg.pt(u+a,floor+y,d);brickRing(pt,profile,.22,.085);
  for(const k of[-1,1])brickJamb(pt,k,w,profileY(profile,k*w/2),.085);
  if(side>0){
   for(let i=0;i<profile.length-1;i++){const A=profile[i],B=profile[i+1];quad(stat,pt(...A,0),pt(...B,0),pt(...B,-b.d),pt(...A,-b.d),i%3?0xb6a68b:0xc8b89a,15)}
   for(const k of[-1,1]){const x=k*w/2,hh=profileY(profile,x);quad(stat,pt(x,0,0),pt(x,hh,0),pt(x,hh,-b.d),pt(x,0,-b.d),0xb4a58e,1)}
  }
  OPENING_RECORDS.push({x:WALK_BRIDGE.x,z,angle,type:'passage',y:floor,w,h,depth:-b.d,profile,du:0,elevated:true});
 }
 for(const side of[-1,1])box(stat,b.x+side*(b.w/2-.11),b.top/2,b.z,.22,b.top,b.d+.045,0xbcae94,0,1);
 box(stat,b.x,b.top+.045,b.z,b.w+.03,.09,b.d+.06,0xc8bca5,0,7);
 box(stat,WALK_BRIDGE.x,floor-.045,b.z,w+.12,.09,b.d+.14,0xd0c4ac,0,7);
 plaque(b.x-1.05,4.6,b.z+b.d/2+.03,1.0,.66,0,12);
}

function closureDetail(b){
 // The marked enclosure has a finished base, tied corner and a weathering cap.
 box(stat,b.x,.22,b.z,b.w+.02,.44,b.d+.02,0x918b7c,0,7);
 box(stat,b.x,b.top-.13,b.z,b.w+.045,.12,b.d+.045,0xb0a38e,0,1);
 if(b.w>b.d){
  for(const x of[b.x-b.w/2+.14,b.x+b.w/2-.14])box(stat,x,b.top/2,b.z,.28,b.top,b.d+.055,0xb9ab92,0,1);
  plaque(b.x,b.top-.9,b.z+b.d/2+.045,1.0,.66,0,12);
 }
}

buildVaults();
// Geometric inlay on selected existing bazaar vaults; the A–B opening remains roofless.
for(const [cx,cz,radius]of[[15.5,46.6,1.30],[32,41.8,1.45],[50.7,26,1.45],[64.4,61.7,1.50],[41.8,75,1.05]]){
 const roof=roofAt(cx,cz);if(!roof)continue;
 const pt=(a,r)=>[cx+Math.cos(a)*r,cz+Math.sin(a)*r];
 const join=(a,b)=>{
  for(let j=0;j<4;j++){
   const point=t=>{const x=a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*t,z=a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*t;return[x,vaultHeightAt(roof,x,z)-.025,z]};
   const p=point(j/4),q=point((j+1)/4);if(!polygonContains(roof.polygon,p[0],p[2])||!polygonContains(roof.polygon,q[0],q[2]))continue;
   beam(stat,p,q,.016,0xe7dfcc,-1,4);
  }
 };
 for(let k=0;k<16;k++){
  const a=k*TAU/16,b=(k+1)*TAU/16;join(pt(a,radius*(k%2?.765:1)),pt(b,radius*((k+1)%2?.765:1)));
 }
 for(let k=0;k<8;k++){
  const a=k*TAU/8,b=(k+1)*TAU/8;join(pt(a,radius*.44),pt(b,radius*.44));join(pt(a,radius*.44),pt(a+Math.PI/8,radius*.765));join(pt(a,radius*1.15),pt(b,radius*1.15));
 }
}


const FACADE_BAYS=[],FACADE_SURFACES=[],OPENING_RECORDS=[],JOIN_STATS={corners:0,bricks:0,vaultSeals:0};
function archPoints(w,h,profile=null,spacing=.19){
 const fn=profile||((u)=>archCurve(u,w,h)),half=[],steps=80;
 for(let i=0;i<=steps;i++)half.push([-w/2+w/2*i/steps,fn(-w/2+w/2*i/steps)]);
 const lengths=[0];for(let i=1;i<half.length;i++)lengths.push(lengths[i-1]+Math.hypot(half[i][0]-half[i-1][0],half[i][1]-half[i-1][1]));
 const n=Math.max(5,Math.ceil(lengths.at(-1)/spacing)),out=[];
 for(let k=0;k<=n;k++){const d=lengths.at(-1)*k/n;let j=1;while(j<lengths.length-1&&lengths[j]<d)j++;const t=(d-lengths[j-1])/(lengths[j]-lengths[j-1]||1);out.push([half[j-1][0]+(half[j][0]-half[j-1][0])*t,half[j-1][1]+(half[j][1]-half[j-1][1])*t])}
 return out.concat(out.slice(0,-1).reverse().map(p=>[-p[0],p[1]]));
}
function profileY(points,u){for(let i=1;i<points.length;i++)if(u<=points[i][0]+1e-7){const a=points[i-1],b=points[i],t=clamp((u-a[0])/(b[0]-a[0]||1),0,1);return a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*t}return points.at(-1)[1]}
function brickRing(pt,points,thickness=.22,depth=.065,offset=0){
 const normals=points.map((p,i)=>{const a=points[Math.max(0,i-1)],b=points[Math.min(points.length-1,i+1)],dx=b[0]-a[0],dy=b[1]-a[1],n=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;return[-dy/n,dx/n]});
 for(let i=0;i<points.length-1;i++){
  const a=points[i],b=points[i+1],na=normals[i],nb=normals[i+1],mix=(a,b,t)=>a.map((v,k)=>v+(b[k]-v)*t),step=Math.hypot(b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1]),g=Math.min(.18,.009/(step||1));
  const A=a.map((v,k)=>v+na[k]*offset),B=b.map((v,k)=>v+nb[k]*offset),D=a.map((v,k)=>v+na[k]*(offset+thickness)),C=b.map((v,k)=>v+nb[k]*(offset+thickness));
  quad(stat,pt(...A,.012),pt(...B,.012),pt(...C,.012),pt(...D,.012),0x9c9687,7);
  const poly=[mix(A,B,g),mix(B,A,g),mix(C,D,g),mix(D,C,g)],d=depth+((i*7)%5-2)*.0025,colors=[0xc4b69d,0xd1c0a2,0xb7a88b,0xcabb9e,0xd8c7a7],col=colors[i%colors.length];
  const P=poly.map((v,k)=>pt(v[0]+(brickNoise(i*13+k)-.5)*.009,v[1]+(brickNoise(i*31+k)-.5)*.007,d+(brickNoise(i*19+k)-.5)*.006)),Q=poly.map(v=>pt(...v,.018));
  quad(stat,P[0],P[1],P[2],P[3],col,15);
  for(const j of[0,2])quad(stat,Q[j],P[j],P[(j+1)%4],Q[(j+1)%4],col,15);
  JOIN_STATS.bricks++;
 }
}
function brickJamb(pt,side,w,spring,depth=.065){
 const count=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(spring/.12)),hh=spring/count;
 for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
  const u0=side<0?-w/2-.22:w/2,u1=u0+.22,y0=i*hh+.006,y1=(i+1)*hh-.006,d=depth+(brickNoise(i*17+side*31)-.5)*.012;
  quad(stat,pt(u0,y0,d),pt(u1,y0,d),pt(u1,y1,d),pt(u0,y1,d),i%3?0xc9b99b:0xb7a68a,15);
  const u=side<0?u1:u0;quad(stat,pt(u,y0,.005),pt(u,y0,d),pt(u,y1,d),pt(u,y1,.005),0xb6a68b,15);JOIN_STATS.bricks++;
 }
}
function agedTimber(pt,w,h,top,depth=-.31,seed=0){
 const n=Math.max(6,Math.round(w/.18)),spring=Math.min(top(-w/2),top(w/2)),wood=[0x888170,0x736d60,0x928877,0x7d7465];
 // A dark solid rear leaf closes the joints; light cannot leak through the aged boards.
 for(let j=0;j<n;j++){const a=-w/2+j*w/n,b=a+w/n;quad(stat,pt(a,0,depth-.014),pt(b,0,depth-.014),pt(b,top(b),depth-.014),pt(a,top(a),depth-.014),0x262622,-1)}
 for(let j=0;j<n;j++){
  const a=-w/2+j*w/n+.006,b=-w/2+(j+1)*w/n-.006,ha=top(a)-.035,hb=top(b)-.035,d=depth+(j%3-1)*.004;
  quad(stat,pt(a,.025,d),pt(b,.025,d),pt(b,hb,d),pt(a,ha,d),wood[(j+seed)%wood.length],4,[[j/n,1],[(j+1)/n,1],[(j+1)/n,0],[j/n,0]]);
 }
 // Frames and inset timber panels are seated inside the reveal, with worn iron straps and studs.
 for(const side of[-1,1]){
  const cx=side*w*.25,pw=w*.39;
  for(const f of[.30,.72]){
   const yy=spring*f,ph=spring*.29;
   quad(stat,pt(cx-pw/2,yy-ph/2,depth+.009),pt(cx+pw/2,yy-ph/2,depth+.009),pt(cx+pw/2,yy+ph/2,depth+.009),pt(cx-pw/2,yy+ph/2,depth+.009),0x625d51,4);
   for(const x of[cx-pw/2,cx+pw/2])quad(stat,pt(x-.019,yy-ph/2-.02,depth+.03),pt(x+.019,yy-ph/2-.02,depth+.03),pt(x+.019,yy+ph/2+.02,depth+.03),pt(x-.019,yy+ph/2+.02,depth+.03),0xa19680,4);
   for(const v of[yy-ph/2,yy+ph/2])quad(stat,pt(cx-pw/2-.02,v-.02,depth+.03),pt(cx+pw/2+.02,v-.02,depth+.03),pt(cx+pw/2+.02,v+.02,depth+.03),pt(cx-pw/2-.02,v+.02,depth+.03),0x9b907c,4);
  }
  for(const v of[.22,spring*.54,spring-.10]){
   quad(stat,pt(cx-w*.215,v-.035,depth+.041),pt(cx+w*.215,v-.035,depth+.041),pt(cx+w*.215,v+.035,depth+.041),pt(cx-w*.215,v+.035,depth+.041),0x3e3d37,5);
   for(const off of[-.17,0,.17]){const u=cx+off*w;quad(stat,pt(u-.013,v-.013,depth+.044),pt(u+.013,v-.013,depth+.044),pt(u+.013,v+.013,depth+.044),pt(u-.013,v+.013,depth+.044),0xb1a48b,-1)}
  }
  const u=side*.13,yy=spring*.59;
  if(side<0)for(let k=0;k<10;k++){const a=k*TAU/10,b=(k+1)*TAU/10;beam(stat,pt(u+Math.cos(a)*.052,yy+Math.sin(a)*.069,depth+.075),pt(u+Math.cos(b)*.052,yy+Math.sin(b)*.069,depth+.075),.012,0x393a34,-1,4)}
  else beam(stat,pt(u,yy-.085,depth+.07),pt(u,yy+.085,depth+.07),.018,0x3b3a34,-1,4);
 }
}
const GRAFFITI_PLAN=[],SITE_LABELS=[];
const PORTAL_PIERS=PORTALS.flatMap(g=>[-1,1].map(side=>({x:g.x+Math.cos(g.angle)*side*(g.width/2+.16),z:g.z-Math.sin(g.angle)*side*(g.width/2+.16),w:.43,d:g.depth+.16,rotation:g.angle,fixedBottom:g.ground,top:g.ground+g.height,kind:'portalPier'})));
const STRUCTURAL_PIERS=[...VAULT_SUPPORTS,...PORTAL_PIERS];
const PORTAL_BOUNDS=PORTALS.map(g=>({x:g.x,z:g.z,w:g.width+.65,d:g.depth+.20,rotation:g.angle,fixedBottom:g.ground,top:g.ground+g.height+.2,kind:'portal'}));
const FACADE_FIT={shifted:0,omitted:0,upperDoors:0};
function projectedProp(seg,b,pad=0){
 const ux=Math.cos(seg.angle),uz=-Math.sin(seg.angle),cs=b.cs??Math.cos(b.rotation||0),sn=b.sn??Math.sin(b.rotation||0),dx=b.x-seg.x,dz=b.z-seg.z;
 const hu=(Math.abs(ux*cs-uz*sn)*b.w+Math.abs(ux*sn+uz*cs)*b.d)/2+pad,hn=(Math.abs(seg.nx*cs-seg.nz*sn)*b.w+Math.abs(seg.nx*sn+seg.nz*cs)*b.d)/2+pad;
 return {u:dx*ux+dz*uz,d:dx*seg.nx+dz*seg.nz,hu,hn,y0:b.fixedBottom??b.foundation??groundAt(b.x,b.z),y1:b.top};
}
function pierIntervals(seg,y0,y1){
 return STRUCTURAL_PIERS.map(b=>projectedProp(seg,b,.085)).filter(b=>Math.abs(b.d)<b.hn+.7&&y0<b.y1+.15&&y1>b.y0-.1).map(b=>[b.u-b.hu,b.u+b.hu]);
}
function roofOccludesOpening(seg,o,u){
 for(const du of[-o.w*.45,0,o.w*.45])for(const d of[.06,.18,.4,.68]){
  const q=seg.pt(u+du,o.y,d),r=roofAt(q[0],q[2]);if(!r)continue;
  const h=vaultHeightAt(r,q[0],q[2]);if(o.y+o.h>h-.25&&o.y<h+.5)return true;
 }
 return false;
}
function fitFacadeOpenings(seg,bays){
 // A second, framed upper door gives the bridge a believable building entrance.
 const bx=WALK_BRIDGE.x,bz=425*MAP_SCALE,du=(bx-seg.x)*Math.cos(seg.angle)-(bz-seg.z)*Math.sin(seg.angle),d=(bx-seg.x)*seg.nx+(bz-seg.z)*seg.nz;
 if(Math.abs(d)<.025&&Math.abs(du)<seg.length/2-.85&&seg.nz<-.8){
  for(const bay of bays)bay.openings=bay.openings.filter(o=>Math.abs(o.u-du)>o.w/2+WALK_BRIDGE.doorW/2+.25||o.y+o.h<3.75);
  const bay=bays.reduce((a,b)=>Math.abs(b.u-du)<Math.abs(a.u-du)?b:a,bays[0]);
  if(bay){bay.hero=false;bay.openings.push({type:'door',u:du,y:WALK_BRIDGE.deck,w:WALK_BRIDGE.doorW,h:WALK_BRIDGE.doorH,arched:true,depth:-.34,elevated:true,profile:archPoints(WALK_BRIDGE.doorW,WALK_BRIDGE.doorH)});FACADE_FIT.upperDoors++}
 }
 for(const bay of bays){
  const result=[];
  for(const o of bay.openings){
   const margin=o.type==='vent'?.06:.27,half=o.w/2+margin,limits=[Math.max(-seg.length/2+.06,bay.u-bay.bw/2+.06)+half,Math.min(seg.length/2-.06,bay.u+bay.bw/2-.06)-half],obstacles=pierIntervals(seg,o.y-.12,o.y+o.h+.25);
   const free=u=>!roofOccludesOpening(seg,o,u)&&u>=limits[0]-1e-5&&u<=limits[1]+1e-5&&!obstacles.some(([a,b])=>u+half>a&&u-half<b)&&!result.some(p=>o.y<p.y+p.h+.2&&o.y+o.h>p.y-.2&&Math.abs(u-p.u)<(o.w+p.w)/2+margin);
   const choices=o.elevated?[o.u]:[o.u,...Array.from({length:32},(_,i)=>o.u+(i%2?1:-1)*(Math.floor(i/2)+1)*.08)];
   const u=o.elevated&&o.u-o.w/2-.25>-seg.length/2&&o.u+o.w/2+.25<seg.length/2?o.u:choices.find(free);
   if(u===undefined){FACADE_FIT.omitted++;bay.hero=false;continue}
   if(Math.abs(u-o.u)>.001){FACADE_FIT.shifted++;bay.hero=false}o.u=u;result.push(o);
  }
  bay.openings=result;if(bay.hero){const d=result.find(o=>o.type==='door');if(!d||pierIntervals(seg,d.y,d.y+5.3).some(([a,b])=>d.u+d.w/2+.55>a&&d.u-d.w/2-.55<b))bay.hero=false}bay.blank=!result.some(o=>o.type!=='vent');

 }
}
function reserveOrnament(bay,y,h,w){ARCH_ORNAMENTS.push({x:bay.x,z:bay.z,angle:bay.angle,y:bay.base+y,w,h})}

function gerehFits(bay){return !GRAFFITI_PLAN.some(g=>g.seg===bay.segment&&Math.abs(g.u-bay.u)<g.w/2+1.2&&g.y+g.h/2>bay.base+2.7&&g.y-g.h/2<bay.base+3.9)&&bay.openings.some(o=>o.type==='door')&&!pierIntervals(bay.segment,bay.base+2.9,bay.base+3.8).some(([a,b])=>bay.u+1.15>a&&bay.u-1.15<b)}

function reserveGraffitiPanels(){
 const anchors=[[35,90],[15,22],[75,19],[40,52],[82,46],[15,47]];
 for(let i=0;i<anchors.length;i++){
  const choices=labelCandidates(anchors[i],2.5,1.65,null,true);
  for(const g of choices){
   const overlaps=(g.seg.holes||[]).filter(o=>g.y-g.h/2-.32<o.y+o.h+.25&&g.y+g.h/2+.32>o.y-.15&&Math.abs(g.u-o.u)<(g.w+o.w)/2+.32);
   if(overlaps.some(o=>o.elevated)){g.score+=10000;continue}
   g.score+=overlaps.length*6;g.remove=overlaps;
  }
  choices.sort((a,b)=>a.score-b.score);const g=choices.find(g=>g.remove);if(!g)continue;
  g.textIndex=i;GRAFFITI_PLAN.push(g);SIGN_PLACEMENTS.push({...g,kind:'reservation'});
  for(const bay of g.seg.bays){
   bay.openings=bay.openings.filter(o=>!g.remove.includes(o));bay.blank=!bay.openings.some(o=>o.type!=='vent');
   if(Math.abs(bay.u-g.u)<g.w/2+bay.bw/2)bay.hero=false;
  }
  g.seg.holes=g.seg.bays.flatMap(b=>b.openings);
 }
 SIGN_PLACEMENTS.length=0;
 for(const seg of FACADE_SURFACES)for(const bay of seg.bays||[])for(const o of bay.openings)OPENING_RECORDS.push({x:bay.x,z:bay.z,angle:seg.angle,type:o.type,y:o.y,w:o.w,h:o.h,depth:o.depth,profile:o.profile,du:o.u-bay.u,elevated:!!o.elevated});
}
// Clip each painted callout to the paving's own triangles. Its vertices and
// normals follow the slope without a hovering quad or per-frame projection.
function groundDecal(cx,cz,r,mat){
 const bounds=[[0,cx-r,1],[0,cx+r,-1],[1,cz-r,1],[1,cz+r,-1]];
 const clip=(poly,axis,limit,sign)=>{
  const out=[];for(let i=0;i<poly.length;i++){
   const a=poly[i],b=poly[(i+1)%poly.length],da=(a[axis]-limit)*sign,db=(b[axis]-limit)*sign;
   if(da>=-1e-8)out.push(a);
   if((da>=0)!==(db>=0)){const t=da/(da-db);out.push([a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*t,a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*t]);}
  }return out;
 };
 for(let z=Math.floor(cz-r);z<cz+r;z++)for(let x=Math.floor(cx-r);x<cx+r;x++){
  for(let poly of [[[x,z],[x,z+1],[x+1,z]],[[x,z+1],[x+1,z+1],[x+1,z]]]){
   for(const [axis,limit,sign]of bounds)poly=clip(poly,axis,limit,sign);
   for(let i=1;i+1<poly.length;i++){
    const pts=[poly[0],poly[i],poly[i+1]],p=pts.map(([u,v])=>[u,floorCell(x,z,u,v)+.018,v]);
    const uv=pts.map(([u,v])=>[.001+.998*(u-cx+r)/(2*r),.001+.998*(v-cz+r)/(2*r)]);
    quad(stat,p[0],p[1],p[2],p[2],0xffffff,mat,[uv[0],uv[1],uv[2],uv[2]]);
   }
  }
 }
}

function drawSiteLabels(){
 for(const s of sites){
  let best=null;
  for(const r of[1.25,1.05,.85,.65]){
   const candidates=[];
   for(let dx=-4;dx<=4;dx+=.25)for(let dz=-4;dz<=4;dz+=.25){
    const x=s.x+dx,z=s.z+dz;
    if(![-r,r].every(a=>[-r,r].every(b=>polygonContains(s.polygon,x+a,z+b)))||cover.some(b=>inCover(b,x,z,r+.16)))continue;
    candidates.push({x,z,r,score:Math.hypot(dx,dz)});
   }
   if(candidates.length){best=candidates.sort((a,b)=>a.score-b.score)[0];break}
  }
  if(!best)continue;
  const {x,z,r}=best,y=groundAt(x,z)+.028;
  groundDecal(x,z,r,s.name==='A'?8:9);
  SITE_LABELS.push({name:s.name,x,y,z,r});
 }
}

function makeBay(seg,u,index,bw){
 const center=seg.pt(u,0,0),floorAt=q=>{const p=seg.pt(q,0,.18);return groundAt(p[0],p[2])},base=floorAt(u),height=seg.top-base,covered=!!roofAt(center[0]+seg.nx*.4,center[2]+seg.nz*.4),w=Math.min(2.1,bw-.68),bay={...seg,u,index,bw,x:center[0],z:center[2],base,covered,openings:[]};
 const add=(type,du,y,ww,hh,arched=false)=>{
  const o={type,u:u+du,y,w:ww,h:hh,arched,depth:type==='vent'?-.16:type==='door'?-.34:-.29};o.profile=arched?archPoints(ww,hh):[[-ww/2,hh],[ww/2,hh]];bay.openings.push(o);return o;
 };
 if(w<.8)return bay;
 bay.blank=index%13===5;
 if(!bay.blank){
  const door=covered||![2,5].includes(index%7),ww=door?w*.89:Math.min(1.15,w*.66),levels=[floorAt(u-ww/2-.10),base,floorAt(u+ww/2+.10)],threshold=Math.max(...levels)+.045;
  if(door&&Math.max(...levels)-Math.min(...levels)<.44){add('door',0,threshold,ww,covered?2.85:index%7===0?3.12:2.72,true);bay.hero=!covered&&index%7===0&&height>5.8}
  else add('window',0,base+1.42,ww,1.35,index%7===5);
 }
 if(!bay.blank&&!bay.hero&&!covered&&height>6.7&&index%3===1)add('window',0,seg.top-2.7,.92,1.24);
 if(height>5&&index%4===2)for(let k=-1;k<=1;k++)add('vent',k*.20,seg.top-1.05,.075,.30);
 return bay;
}
function recessOpening(seg,o,index){
 const p=(u,v,d)=>seg.pt(o.u+u,o.y+v,d),pts=o.profile,back=o.depth;
 // Soffits and jamb returns connect the carved wall opening to the recessed leaf or screen.
 for(let i=0;i<pts.length-1;i++){const a=pts[i],b=pts[i+1];quad(stat,p(...a,0),p(...b,0),p(b[0]*.97,b[1]-.035,-.085),p(a[0]*.97,a[1]-.035,-.085),0xc0b196,15);quad(stat,p(a[0]*.97,a[1]-.035,-.085),p(b[0]*.97,b[1]-.035,-.085),p(...b,back),p(...a,back),0xa89a80,15)}
 for(const side of[-1,1]){const u=side*o.w/2,hh=profileY(pts,u);quad(stat,p(u,0,0),p(u,hh,0),p(u,hh,back),p(u,0,back),side<0?0xb8ab91:0x8d826f,1)}
 quad(stat,p(-o.w/2,0,0),p(o.w/2,0,0),p(o.w/2,0,back),p(-o.w/2,0,back),0xb6aa92,7);
 if(o.type==='vent'){
  quad(stat,p(-o.w/2,0,back),p(o.w/2,0,back),p(o.w/2,o.h,back),p(-o.w/2,o.h,back),0x292c28,-1);return;
 }
 if(o.arched){brickRing(p,pts,.22,.07);for(const side of[-1,1])brickJamb(p,side,o.w,profileY(pts,side*o.w/2),.067)}
 else{
  for(const side of[-1,1])brickJamb(p,side,o.w,o.h,.060);
  box(stat,...p(0,o.h+.08,.045),o.w+.44,.16,.17,0xc9b99b,seg.angle,15);
 }
 // Recessed timber frame around the leaf; the frame itself has visible depth.
 for(const side of[-1,1]){const u=side*(o.w/2-.038),hh=profileY(pts,u);box(stat,...p(u,hh/2,back+.045),.075,hh,.085,0x574f42,seg.angle,4)}
 for(let i=0;i<pts.length-1;i++){const a=pts[i],b=pts[i+1];quad(stat,p(a[0],a[1],back+.045),p(b[0],b[1],back+.045),p(b[0],b[1]-.075,back+.045),p(a[0],a[1]-.075,back+.045),0x6e6250,4)}
 if(o.type==='door'){
  agedTimber(p,o.w-.09,o.h,u=>profileY(pts,u)-.035,back+.015,index);
  // A stone threshold closes down to the actual paving, including sloping alleys.
  const w=o.w+.30,zFront=.23,zBack=back-.02,basePoint=(u,d)=>{const q=seg.pt(o.u+u,0,d);return o.elevated?o.y-.14:groundAt(q[0],q[2])-.015};
  const paving=[p(-w/2,-.015,zFront),p(w/2,-.015,zFront),p(w/2,-.015,zBack),p(-w/2,-.015,zBack)];quad(stat,...paving,0xffffff,2,paving.map(q=>[q[0]/3,q[2]/3]));
  quad(stat,p(-w/2,basePoint(-w/2,zFront)-o.y,zFront),p(w/2,basePoint(w/2,zFront)-o.y,zFront),p(w/2,-.015,zFront),p(-w/2,-.015,zFront),0xa89c85,7);
  for(const side of[-1,1]){const u=side*w/2;quad(stat,p(u,basePoint(u,zFront)-o.y,zFront),p(u,basePoint(u,zBack)-o.y,zBack),p(u,-.015,zBack),p(u,-.015,zFront),0xb5a990,7)}
 }else{
  for(let i=0;i<pts.length-1;i++){const a=pts[i],b=pts[i+1];quad(stat,p(a[0],0,back),p(b[0],0,back),p(...b,back),p(...a,back),0x343832,-1)}
  const center=p(0,o.h/2,0);gerehPanel(center[0],center[1],center[2],o.w-.12,o.h-.14,seg.angle,back+.058,false);
  box(stat,...p(0,-.035,-.015),o.w+.32,.11,.36,0xcac0aa,seg.angle,7);

 }
}
function planGerehNiches(){
 for(const seg of FACADE_SURFACES)for(const bay of seg.bays||[]){
  const door=bay.openings.find(o=>o.type==='door');if(!door)continue;
  if(!bay.hero&&!(bay.covered&&bay.index%5===0&&gerehFits(bay)))continue;
  const w=Math.min(door.w+.44,bay.bw-.40),h=bay.hero?.98:.86;
  const n={type:'niche',u:door.u,y:door.y+door.h+.38,w,h,depth:-.58,profile:[[-w/2,h],[w/2,h]],arched:false};
  if(n.u-w/2< -seg.length/2+.12||n.u+w/2>seg.length/2-.12||n.y+h>seg.top-.42)continue;
  if(seg.holes.some(o=>n.y<o.y+o.h+.08&&n.y+h>o.y-.08&&Math.abs(n.u-o.u)<(n.w+o.w)/2+.1))continue;
  if(pierIntervals(seg,n.y,n.y+h).some(([a,b])=>n.u+w/2+.12>a&&n.u-w/2-.12<b))continue;
  seg.holes.push(n);bay.gerehNiche=n;
  OPENING_RECORDS.push({x:bay.x,z:bay.z,angle:seg.angle,type:'niche',y:n.y,w,h,depth:n.depth,profile:n.profile,du:n.u-bay.u});
 }
}
function recessedGereh(bay){
 const n=bay.gerehNiche;if(!n)return;
 const p=(u,v,d)=>bay.pt(n.u+u,n.y+v,d),w=n.w,h=n.h,back=n.depth;
 const corners=[[-w/2,0],[w/2,0],[w/2,h],[-w/2,h]];
 for(let i=0;i<4;i++){const A=corners[i],B=corners[(i+1)%4];quad(stat,p(...A,0),p(...B,0),p(...B,back),p(...A,back),i===2?0x8b806d:0xb6a68c,15)}
 quad(stat,p(-w/2,0,back),p(w/2,0,back),p(w/2,h,back),p(-w/2,h,back),0x786e5d,0);
 // The entire frame sits inside the reveal; its rails end at the side stiles.
 for(const side of[-1,1])box(stat,...p(side*(w/2-.045),h/2,-.095),.09,h,.09,0xcec1a8,bay.angle,15);
 for(const yy of[.045,h-.045])box(stat,...p(0,yy,-.095),w-.18,.09,.09,0xd1c3a9,bay.angle,15);
 const q=p(0,h/2,0);gerehPanel(q[0],q[1],q[2],w-.24,h-.24,bay.angle,back+.075,true);
 ARCH_ORNAMENTS.push({x:q[0],z:q[2],angle:bay.angle,y:n.y+h/2,w,h});
}

function renderBay(bay){
 for(const o of bay.openings)recessOpening(bay,o,bay.index);
 const o=bay.openings.find(o=>o.type==='door');
 if(bay.hero&&o){
  const origin=bay.pt(o.u,o.y,0),p=(u,y,d)=>bay.pt(o.u+u,o.y+y,d),n=bay.gerehNiche;
  const frameHeight=n?n.y+n.h-o.y+.13:o.h+1.45;
  for(const side of[-1,1])box(stat,...p(side*(o.w/2+.30),frameHeight/2,.015),.17,frameHeight,.11,0xc7baa0,bay.angle,1);
  box(stat,...p(0,frameHeight+.065,.025),o.w+.77,.13,.13,0xded4bc,bay.angle,7);
  recessedGereh(bay);
  const upperY=o.y+frameHeight+.52,upperW=Math.min(bay.bw-.35,o.w+.65);
  if(upperY+.31<bay.top-.25){
   ARCH_ORNAMENTS.push({x:origin[0],z:origin[2],angle:bay.angle,y:upperY,w:upperW,h:.62});
   gerehPanel(origin[0],upperY,origin[2],upperW,.62,bay.angle,.025,true);
  }
  plaque(origin[0],o.y+3.0,origin[2],.52,.22,bay.angle,12);
 }else if(bay.covered&&bay.index%5===0&&gerehFits(bay)){recessedGereh(bay)}
}

function masonryFace(seg,holes){
 const edges=[-seg.length/2,seg.length/2];for(const o of holes){edges.push(o.u-o.w/2,o.u+o.w/2);for(const p of o.profile)edges.push(o.u+p[0])}
 const xs=[...new Set(edges.map(x=>Math.round(x*1e7)/1e7))].sort((a,b)=>a-b);
 function panel(a,b,lo0,lo1,hi0,hi1){
  if(hi0<=lo0+.00001&&hi1<=lo1+.00001)return;
  const point=(u,y)=>seg.pt(u,y,0),floor=u=>{const q=seg.pt(u,0,.15);return groundAt(q[0],q[2])+.38};
  const m0=clamp(floor(a),lo0,hi0),m1=clamp(floor(b),lo1,hi1);
  for(const [y0,y1,t0,t1,mat,col]of[[lo0,lo1,m0,m1,1,0xc0b49b],[m0,m1,hi0,hi1,seg.material??0,seg.tint]]){
   if(t0<=y0+.00001&&t1<=y1+.00001)continue;
   quad(stat,point(a,y0),point(b,y1),point(b,t1),point(a,t0),col,mat,[[a/3,-y0/3],[b/3,-y1/3],[b/3,-t1/3],[a/3,-t0/3]]);
  }
 }
 for(let i=1;i<xs.length;i++){
  const a=xs[i-1],b=xs[i],mid=(a+b)/2;if(b-a<1e-6)continue;
  const active=holes.filter(o=>mid>o.u-o.w/2&&mid<o.u+o.w/2).sort((a,b)=>a.y-b.y);let lo0=-2,lo1=-2;
  for(const o of active){panel(a,b,lo0,lo1,o.y,o.y);lo0=o.y+profileY(o.profile,a-o.u);lo1=o.y+profileY(o.profile,b-o.u)}panel(a,b,lo0,lo1,seg.top,seg.top);
 }
}
function continuousCoping(poly,top){
 const n=poly.length;
 const normal=(a,b)=>{const dx=b[0]-a[0],dz=b[1]-a[1],l=Math.hypot(dx,dz)||1;let nx=-dz/l,nz=dx/l;if(wall((a[0]+b[0])/2+nx*.25,(a[1]+b[1])/2+nz*.25)){nx=-nx;nz=-nz}return[nx,nz]};
 const normals=poly.map((a,i)=>normal(a,poly[(i+1)%n]));
 const offset=(i,d)=>{const a=normals[(i+n-1)%n],b=normals[i],den=Math.max(.12,1+a[0]*b[0]+a[1]*b[1]);let dx=(a[0]+b[0])*d/den,dz=(a[1]+b[1])*d/den;const l=Math.hypot(dx,dz),limit=Math.max(.18,Math.abs(d)*2.4);if(l>limit){dx*=limit/l;dz*=limit/l}return[poly[i][0]+dx,poly[i][1]+dz]};
 for(const [profile,mat,col]of[[[[-.14,-.07],[.19,-.07],[.19,.10],[-.14,.10]],7,0xd8ceb8],[[[-.08,-.21],[.12,-.21],[.12,-.10],[-.08,-.10]],1,0xb4a58c]]){
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const j=(i+1)%n;for(let k=0;k<profile.length;k++){
   const a=profile[k],b=profile[(k+1)%profile.length],pt=(idx,p)=>{const q=offset(idx,p[0]);return[q[0],top+p[1],q[1]]};
   quad(stat,pt(i,a),pt(j,a),pt(j,b),pt(i,b),col,mat);
  }}
 }
 JOIN_STATS.corners+=n;
}
function tracedWalls(){let index=0;
 for(let ring=0;ring<MAP_LOOPS.length;ring++){
  const poly=MAP_LOOPS[ring],top=Math.max(...poly.map(p=>groundAt(...p)))+6.8+(ring%3)*.65;
  for(let i=0;i<poly.length;i++){
   const a=poly[i],b=poly[(i+1)%poly.length],dx=b[0]-a[0],dz=b[1]-a[1],length=Math.hypot(dx,dz);if(length<.03)continue;
   let nx=-dz/length,nz=dx/length;if(wall((a[0]+b[0])/2+nx*.3,(a[1]+b[1])/2+nz*.3)){nx=-nx;nz=-nz}
   const x=(a[0]+b[0])/2,z=(a[1]+b[1])/2,angle=Math.atan2(nx,nz),seg={x,z,nx,nz,angle,length,top,tint:[0xf2eddf,0xe6ddc8,0xece4d5][ring%3],pt:(u,y,d=0)=>facePoint(x,z,angle,u,y,d)};
   const count=length>=2.2?Math.max(1,Math.floor(length/3.4)):0,bays=[];
   for(let k=0;k<count;k++){const bay=makeBay(seg,-length/2+(k+.5)*length/count,index++,length/count);bays.push(bay);FACADE_BAYS.push(bay)}
   fitFacadeOpenings(seg,bays);seg.holes=bays.flatMap(b=>b.openings);seg.bays=bays;for(const bay of bays)bay.segment=seg;FACADE_SURFACES.push(seg);
  }
  continuousCoping(poly,top);if(ring>0)roofPolygon(poly,top);
 }
 reserveGraffitiPanels();planGerehNiches();
 for(const seg of FACADE_SURFACES){masonryFace(seg,seg.holes);for(const bay of seg.bays)renderBay(bay)}
}
function buildTurnBearings(){
 const r=ROOFS[2],y=r.bearing,north=335*MAP_SCALE,south=414*MAP_SCALE,east=830*MAP_SCALE;
 // Solid spring-course returns at each end of the recessed mouth.
 for(const z of[north,south]){
  box(stat,east+.16,y-.10,z,.72,.20,.40,0xb9ad93,0,1);
  box(stat,east+.16,y+.025,z,.78,.07,.46,0xd2c4a9,0,7);
 }
 // A solid stepped spring-course connects the recessed crown to the side wall.
 // Its soffit is above the open lane; the crown remains behind the wall face.
 const wallFace=56.62,base=y-.02,n=Math.ceil((south-north)/.18);
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
  const za=north+(south-north)*i/n,zb=north+(south-north)*(i+1)/n;
  const ya=vaultHeightAt(r,east,za)+.008,yb=vaultHeightAt(r,east,zb)+.008;
  const A=[east-.012,ya,za],B=[east-.012,yb,zb],C=[wallFace,yb,zb],D=[wallFace,ya,za];
  quad(stat,A,D,C,B,0xc7b99d,1);
  quad(stat,[east-.012,base,za],[east-.012,base,zb],B,A,0xc7b99d,1,[[za/2.6,base/2.6],[zb/2.6,base/2.6],[zb/2.6,yb/2.6],[za/2.6,ya/2.6]]);
  quad(stat,[wallFace,base,zb],[wallFace,base,za],D,C,0xc7b99d,1);
  quad(stat,[east-.012,base,zb],[east-.012,base,za],[wallFace,base,za],[wallFace,base,zb],0xb9ad93,1);
 }
 // A level shoulder closes the diagonal wall junction below the vault.
 for(let x=760*MAP_SCALE;x<east;x+=.36){
  const len=Math.min(.36,east-x),z=south+(east-x)*(31/70);
  box(stat,x+len/2,y-.12,z,len+.004,.24,.26,0xb9ad93,0,1);
 }
}
function sealVaultJunctions(){
 const edges=MAP_LOOPS.flatMap(loop=>loop.map((a,i)=>[a,loop[(i+1)%loop.length]]));
 const nearest=(x,z,dx,dz)=>{let best=null,distance=.58;for(const[a,b]of edges){const vx=b[0]-a[0],vz=b[1]-a[1],len=Math.hypot(vx,vz);if(!len||Math.abs((vx*dx+vz*dz)/len)<.82)continue;const t=clamp(((x-a[0])*vx+(z-a[1])*vz)/(len*len),0,1),q=[a[0]+vx*t,a[1]+vz*t],d=Math.hypot(q[0]-x,q[1]-z);if(d<distance){distance=d;best=q}}return best};
 for(const roof of ROOFS)for(let i=0;i<roof.polygon.length;i++){
  const a=roof.polygon[i],b=roof.polygon[(i+1)%roof.polygon.length],len=Math.hypot(b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1]);if(len<.04)continue;const dx=(b[0]-a[0])/len,dz=(b[1]-a[1])/len,n=Math.ceil(len/.5);
  for(let k=0;k<n;k++){
   const p=t=>[a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*t,a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*t],aa=p(k/n),bb=p((k+1)/n),qa=nearest(...aa,dx,dz),qb=nearest(...bb,dx,dz);if(!qa||!qb)continue;
   const y0=vaultHeightAt(roof,...aa),y1=vaultHeightAt(roof,...bb),A=[aa[0],y0-.008,aa[1]],B=[bb[0],y1-.008,bb[1]],C=[qb[0],y1+.04,qb[1]],D=[qa[0],y0+.04,qa[1]];
   quad(stat,A,B,C,D,0xc9bca2,1);quad(stat,D,C,[qb[0],y1+.22,qb[1]],[qa[0],y0+.22,qa[1]],0xbcae94,1);JOIN_STATS.vaultSeals++;
  }
 }
}

tracedWalls();
sealVaultJunctions();
buildTurnBearings();

// Props use the same oriented footprints for rendering, movement, bullets and navigation.
function parapet(b){
 const x0=b.x-b.w/2,x1=b.x+b.w/2,z0=b.z-b.d/2,z1=b.z+b.d/2;
 const y=b.fixedBottom??groundAt(b.x,b.z),p=(x,z,h)=>[x,y+h,z];
 quad(stat,p(x0,z0,0),p(x1,z0,0),p(x1,z0,b.h),p(x0,z0,b.h),0xd2c8b2,0);
 quad(stat,p(x1,z1,0),p(x0,z1,0),p(x0,z1,b.h),p(x1,z1,b.h),0xcac0aa,0);
 quad(stat,p(x0,z1,0),p(x0,z0,0),p(x0,z0,b.h),p(x0,z1,b.h),0xb6a98f,1);
 quad(stat,p(x1,z0,0),p(x1,z1,0),p(x1,z1,b.h),p(x1,z0,b.h),0xb6a98f,1);
 box(stat,b.x,y+b.h-.05,b.z,b.w+.015,.10,b.d+.10,0xe4dac3,0,7);
 for(let center=b.x-b.w/2+.5;center<b.x+b.w/2;center+=1)if(Math.floor(center)%3===0)for(const side of[-1,1])gerehPanel(center,y+.38,b.z+side*(b.d/2+.003),.72,.34,side>0?0:Math.PI,.012,false);
}
function crate(b){
 const by=groundAt(b.x,b.z),layers=b.h>1.65?2:1,hh=(b.h-.08)/layers;
 for(let l=0;l<layers;l++){
  const bottom=by+.08+l*hh,top=bottom+hh-.018,y=(bottom+top)/2,wood=l%2?0x91826a:0xa9997a;
  box(stat,b.x,(bottom+.13+top-.15)/2,b.z,b.w,top-bottom-.28,b.d,wood,0,4,48);
  // Raised, bevelled timber lid; each chest stays inside its original cover bounds.
  box(stat,b.x,top-.075,b.z,b.w,.15,b.d,0xb3a183,0,4,32);
  box(stat,b.x,bottom+.065,b.z,b.w,.13,b.d,0x655b4c,0,4,16);
  for(const [angle,width,depth]of[[0,b.w,b.d/2],[Math.PI,b.w,b.d/2],[Math.PI/2,b.d,b.w/2],[-Math.PI/2,b.d,b.w/2]]){
   const pt=(u,v,d=0)=>facePoint(b.x,b.z,angle,u,bottom+v,depth+d),h=top-bottom;
   const fw=width-.15,fh=h-.28;
   quad(stat,pt(-fw/2,.13,.007),pt(fw/2,.13,.007),pt(fw/2,.13+fh,.007),pt(-fw/2,.13+fh,.007),0xc7b89a,3,[[0,fh/1.2],[fw/1.2,fh/1.2],[fw/1.2,0],[0,0]]);
   // The brand is a separate, fixed-size plaque with its source aspect ratio intact.
   const badgeW=Math.min(.72,fw-.08,(fh-.12)*300/142),badgeH=badgeW*142/300,cy=.13+fh/2;
   if(badgeW>.2)quad(stat,pt(-badgeW/2,cy-badgeH/2,.012),pt(badgeW/2,cy-badgeH/2,.012),pt(badgeW/2,cy+badgeH/2,.012),pt(-badgeW/2,cy+badgeH/2,.012),0xc7b89a,13,[[106/512,308/512],[406/512,308/512],[406/512,166/512],[106/512,166/512]]);
   // Recessed inlay panels are framed by structural rails, not black cargo straps.
   for(const v of[.13,.13+fh])quad(stat,pt(-fw/2-.025,v-.022,.016),pt(fw/2+.025,v-.022,.016),pt(fw/2+.025,v+.022,.016),pt(-fw/2-.025,v+.022,.016),0xb6a385,4);
   for(const u of[-fw/2,fw/2])quad(stat,pt(u-.022,.13,.016),pt(u+.022,.13,.016),pt(u+.022,.13+fh,.016),pt(u-.022,.13+fh,.016),0xb6a385,4);
   // Small forged corners and studs, kept flush so collision remains faithful.
   for(const u of[-width/2+.038,width/2-.038])for(const v of[.12,h-.12]){
    quad(stat,pt(u-.032,v-.08,.019),pt(u+.032,v-.08,.019),pt(u+.032,v+.08,.019),pt(u-.032,v+.08,.019),0x47443d,5);
    quad(stat,pt(u-.012,v-.012,.022),pt(u+.012,v-.012,.022),pt(u+.012,v+.012,.022),pt(u-.012,v+.012,.022),0xb7aa90);
   }
   if(angle===0||angle===Math.PI){
    quad(stat,pt(-.055,h-.24,.024),pt(.055,h-.24,.024),pt(.055,h-.08,.024),pt(-.055,h-.08,.024),0x393932,5);
    for(let k=0;k<8;k++){const a=k*TAU/8,c=(k+1)*TAU/8;quad(stat,pt(Math.cos(a)*.058,h-.24+Math.sin(a)*.066,.04),pt(Math.cos(c)*.058,h-.24+Math.sin(c)*.066,.04),pt(Math.cos(c)*.036,h-.24+Math.sin(c)*.044,.04),pt(Math.cos(a)*.036,h-.24+Math.sin(a)*.044,.04),0xaca28c)}
   }else{
    quad(stat,pt(-.15,h*.51-.016,.035),pt(.15,h*.51-.016,.035),pt(.15,h*.51+.016,.035),pt(-.15,h*.51+.016,.035),0x3e3c35);
    for(const u of[-.13,.13])quad(stat,pt(u-.016,h*.51,.035),pt(u+.016,h*.51,.035),pt(u+.016,h*.57,.014),pt(u-.016,h*.57,.014),0x3e3c35);
   }
  }
  // Girih inlay continues onto the lid, visible from raised walkways.
  quad(stat,[b.x-b.w*.41,top+.002,b.z+b.d*.4],[b.x+b.w*.41,top+.002,b.z+b.d*.4],[b.x+b.w*.41,top+.002,b.z-b.d*.4],[b.x-b.w*.41,top+.002,b.z-b.d*.4],0xa49477,3,[[0,b.d*.8/1.2],[b.w*.82/1.2,b.d*.8/1.2],[b.w*.82/1.2,0],[0,0]]);
 }
 for(const a of[-1,1])for(const c of[-1,1])box(stat,b.x+a*(b.w/2-.14),by+.04,b.z+c*(b.d/2-.14),.20,.08,.20,0x5c5345,0,4);
}
function car(b){
 const meshStart=stat.length,base=groundAt(b.x,b.z),w=b.w,d=b.d,S=b.h/1.45;
 const p=(x,y,z)=>[b.x+x*w,base+y*S,b.z+z*d];
 const Q=(a,c,e,f,col,mat=-1,uv=QUAD_UV)=>quad(stat,p(...a),p(...c),p(...e),p(...f),col,mat,uv);
 const line=(a,c,r,col)=>beam(stat,p(...a),p(...c),r,col,-1,5);
 const paint=0xdcd5c6,shade=0xbdb6a9,chrome=0xd2d5d2,darkChrome=0x969e9d,black=0x202323,glass=0x384346;
 const axles=[-.286,.292],wheelY=.306,R=.306,archR=.342;
 // The side skins have actual wheel cut-outs. No solid box crosses the tyres.
 const halfWidth=z=>.478-.030*Math.pow(Math.abs(z)/.47,6);
 const shoulder=z=>.904-.063*Math.pow(Math.abs(z)/.47,4);
 const archBottom=z=>{let y=.278;for(const c of axles){const q=(z-c)*d;if(Math.abs(q)<archR)y=Math.max(y,wheelY+Math.sqrt(archR*archR-q*q))}return y};
 const cuts=[-.47,.47,...Array.from({length:48},(_,i)=>-.47+i*.94/47)];
 for(const z of axles)for(let k=0;k<=24;k++)cuts.push(z+Math.cos(k*Math.PI/24)*archR/d);
 cuts.sort((a,c)=>a-c);
 for(let i=1;i<cuts.length;i++){const z0=cuts[i-1],z1=cuts[i];if(z1-z0<.00001)continue;for(const s of[-1,1]){
  Q([s*halfWidth(z0),archBottom(z0),z0],[s*halfWidth(z1),archBottom(z1),z1],[s*halfWidth(z1),shoulder(z1),z1],[s*halfWidth(z0),shoulder(z0),z0],s<0?shade:paint);
 }}
 box(stat,b.x,base+.31*S,b.z,w*.70,.09*S,d*.84,0x303330);
 for(const s of[-1,1])for(const z of axles){
  for(let k=0;k<24;k++){const a=k*Math.PI/24,c=(k+1)*Math.PI/24;
   const at=(r,t)=>[s*(halfWidth(z)+.003),wheelY+Math.sin(t)*r,z+Math.cos(t)*r/d];
   Q(at(archR,a),at(archR,c),at(archR+.020,c),at(archR+.020,a),paint);
   Q([s*.418,wheelY,z],at(archR,a).map((v,i)=>i===0?s*.418:v),at(archR,c).map((v,i)=>i===0?s*.418:v),[s*.418,wheelY,z],black);
  }
 }
 // Low, long bonnet with softened shoulders and a separate short rear boot.
 for(const bands of [[[-.47,.84],[-.437,.875],[-.17,.924],[-.146,.917]],[[.326,.908],[.437,.866],[.47,.83]]]){
  for(let j=1;j<bands.length;j++)for(let i=0;i<10;i++){
   const [z0,y0]=bands[j-1],[z1,y1]=bands[j],u0=-1+i*.2,u1=u0+.2;
   const at=(u,z,y)=>[u*halfWidth(z),y-.020*Math.pow(Math.abs(u),6),z];
   Q(at(u0,z0,y0),at(u1,z0,y0),at(u1,z1,y1),at(u0,z1,y1),paint);
  }
 }
 Q([-.448,.28,-.471],[.448,.28,-.471],[.448,.84,-.471],[-.448,.84,-.471],paint);
 Q([.448,.28,.471],[-.448,.28,.471],[-.448,.83,.471],[.448,.83,.471],shade);
 // Roof crown is rounded across the car and toward its front and rear edges.
 const roofs=[[-.020,1.410,.386],[.008,1.440,.394],[.060,1.450,.398],[.166,1.450,.398],[.207,1.425,.387]];
 for(let j=1;j<roofs.length;j++)for(let i=0;i<12;i++){
  const at=(r,u)=>[u*r[2],r[1]-.028*Math.pow(Math.abs(u),4),r[0]],u=-1+i/6;
  Q(at(roofs[j-1],u),at(roofs[j-1],u+1/6),at(roofs[j],u+1/6),at(roofs[j],u),paint);
 }
 const front=[[-.456,.918,-.146],[.456,.918,-.146],[.383,1.412,-.019],[-.383,1.412,-.019]];
 const rear=[[.453,.906,.326],[-.453,.906,.326],[-.383,1.421,.207],[.383,1.421,.207]];
 for(const q of[front,rear]){Q(...q,glass);for(let i=0;i<4;i++){line(q[i],q[(i+1)%4],.022,black);line(q[i],q[(i+1)%4],.010,chrome)}}
 // Quiet sky reflection inside the windscreen; wipers sit on the cowl.
 Q([-.371,1.31,-.046],[.371,1.31,-.046],[.376,1.355,-.035],[-.376,1.355,-.035],0x76807e);
 for(const x of[-.24,.16]){line([x,.93,-.148],[x+.13,1.055,-.113],.006,black);line([x+.06,1.015,-.126],[x+.20,1.105,-.100],.008,black)}
 for(const s of[-1,1]){
  const A=[s*.474,.916,-.142],B=[s*.386,1.410,-.020],C=[s*.387,1.422,.207],D=[s*.474,.908,.326];
  Q(A,D,C,B,glass);const outline=[A,B,C,D,A];for(let i=1;i<outline.length;i++){line(outline[i-1],outline[i],.030,paint);line(outline[i-1],outline[i],.012,chrome)}
  // Two doors and the distinctive small quarterlight at the front.
  line([s*.474,.918,.100],[s*.398,1.443,.100],.025,black);line([s*.474,.918,.100],[s*.398,1.443,.100],.014,chrome);
  line([s*.474,.918,-.063],[s*.398,1.388,-.011],.010,chrome);
  const x=s*.482;
  for(const z of[-.142,.100,.326])line([x,.32,z],[x,.897,z],.004,0x68675f);
  line([x,.319,-.142],[x,.319,.326],.004,0x68675f);
  line([x,.848,-.426],[x,.848,.432],.009,chrome);
  line([x,.307,-.142],[x,.307,.326],.012,chrome);
  for(const z of[.068,.286]){box(stat,...p(x,.820,z),.015,.036*S,.048*d,0x676f6d);line([x+s*.006,.832,z-.018],[x+s*.006,.832,z+.018],.010,chrome)}
  Q([s*.451,1.10,-.055],[s*.451,1.10,.085],[s*.436,1.20,.085],[s*.436,1.20,-.045],0x596565);
  Q([s*.451,1.10,.117],[s*.451,1.10,.268],[s*.436,1.20,.247],[s*.436,1.20,.117],0x596565);
  // Small round chrome mirror on a slim stalk, as on the reference car.
  line([s*.474,.945,-.12],[s*.514,1.015,-.125],.010,chrome);
  const center=p(s*.518,1.04,-.125);for(let k=0;k<20;k++){const a=k*TAU/20,c=(k+1)*TAU/20,pt=t=>[center[0]+Math.cos(t)*.047,center[1]+Math.sin(t)*.041,center[2]];quad(stat,center,pt(a),pt(c),center,k%4?chrome:darkChrome)}
  // Tucked rain gutter and a narrow pressed bonnet crease.
  line([s*.398,1.431,-.017],[s*.400,1.443,.207],.009,chrome);
  line([s*.275,.879,-.429],[s*.257,.928,-.174],.004,0xe9e2d2);
 }
 function roundPanel(cx,cy,z,ww,hh,r,col){
  const ring=[];for(let corner=0;corner<4;corner++)for(let j=0;j<=5;j++){const a=-Math.PI/2+corner*Math.PI/2+j*Math.PI/10,cx0=cx+(corner<2?1:-1)*(ww/2-r/w),cy0=cy+(corner===1||corner===2?1:-1)*(hh/2-r);ring.push([cx0+Math.cos(a)*r/w,cy0+Math.sin(a)*r,z]);}
  for(let i=0;i<ring.length;i++)Q([cx,cy,z],ring[i],ring[(i+1)%ring.length],[cx,cy,z],col);
 }
 // One continuous black grille, surrounded by chrome; no central divider.
 roundPanel(0,.664,-.474,.548,.292,.040,chrome);roundPanel(0,.664,-.477,.528,.266,.030,black);
 for(let i=0;i<9;i++)line([-.252,.548+i*.027,-.480],[.252,.548+i*.027,-.480],.0035,0x8c9593);
 for(let i=0;i<18;i++){const a=.40+i*(TAU-.8)/18,c=.40+(i+1)*(TAU-.8)/18;line([-.169+Math.cos(a)*.063/w,.674+Math.sin(a)*.063,-.486],[-.169+Math.cos(c)*.063/w,.674+Math.sin(c)*.063,-.486],.005,chrome)}
 line([.115,.595,-.486],[.200,.595,-.486],.007,chrome);
 for(const s of[-1,1]){
  const x=s*.363;
  roundPanel(x,.661,-.476,.190,.288,.045,chrome);roundPanel(x,.661,-.480,.169,.259,.038,black);roundPanel(x,.661,-.483,.146,.223,.036,0xd9ded3);
  roundPanel(x,.661,-.485,.117,.171,.024,0x8e9991);roundPanel(x,.665,-.488,.094,.136,.020,0xd8ded3);
  for(let i=0;i<6;i++)line([x-.054+i*.021,.588,-.490],[x-.054+i*.021,.731,-.490],.002,0xe6e7dd);
  roundPanel(s*.343,.325,-.485,.137,.102,.014,chrome);roundPanel(s*.343,.325,-.488,.115,.073,.008,0xa59c7c);
  roundPanel(s*.355,.620,.474,.155,.177,.021,chrome);roundPanel(s*.355,.620,.478,.134,.149,.017,0x62645a);
  line([s*.418,.60,.480],[s*.292,.60,.480],.005,chrome);
 }
 // Shallow, continuous bumpers wrap around the two front corners.
 for(const end of[-1,1]){
  const path=[[-.475,.433],[-.475,.470],[-.440,.491],[-.35,.497],[.35,.497],[.440,.491],[.475,.470],[.475,.433]];
  const profile=[[-.045,-.006],[-.035,.008],[.032,.008],[.045,-.003],[.032,-.015],[-.035,-.015]];
  for(let i=1;i<path.length;i++)for(let j=0;j<profile.length;j++){
   const at=(k,a)=>[path[k][0],.425+profile[a][0],end*(path[k][1]+profile[a][1])];Q(at(i-1,j),at(i,j),at(i,(j+1)%profile.length),at(i-1,(j+1)%profile.length),j===1?chrome:darkChrome);
  }
  for(const x of[-.29,.29]){box(stat,...p(x,.421,end*.494),.045,.14*S,.045,chrome);box(stat,...p(x,.422,end*.502),.026,.10*S,.018,black)}
  const pw=.245,ph=pw*w*(.997-.934)/(.372-.004)/S,cy=.256,z=end*.500;
  Q([-pw/2,cy-ph/2,z],[pw/2,cy-ph/2,z],[pw/2,cy+ph/2,z],[-pw/2,cy+ph/2,z],0xffffff,13,[[.004,.997],[.372,.997],[.372,.934],[.004,.934]]);
 }
 // Rounded tyres, steel trim rings and densely fluted radial hubcaps.
 for(const s of[-1,1])for(const z of axles){
  const at=(x,r,t)=>p(s*x,wheelY+Math.cos(t)*r,z+Math.sin(t)*r/d),profile=[[.416,.19],[.416,.275],[.430,R],[.480,R],[.502,.278],[.505,.194]],segments=32;
  for(let j=1;j<profile.length;j++)for(let k=0;k<segments;k++){const a=k*TAU/segments,c=(k+1)*TAU/segments;quad(stat,at(...profile[j-1],a),at(...profile[j-1],c),at(...profile[j],c),at(...profile[j],a),j===3&&k%2?0x2d302d:black)}
  for(let k=0;k<segments;k++){
   const a=k*TAU/segments,c=(k+1)*TAU/segments;
   for(const [r0,r1,x,col]of[[.192,.207,.506,chrome],[.174,.192,.509,0x7e8784],[.088,.174,.510,black],[0,.088,.516,chrome]])quad(stat,at(x,r0,a),at(x,r0,c),at(x,r1,c),at(x,r1,a),col);
   quad(stat,at(.512,.092,a+.025),at(.512,.174,a+.020),at(.512,.174,c-.05),at(.512,.092,c-.05),k%2?chrome:darkChrome);
  }
  for(let k=0;k<5;k++){const a=k*TAU/5;const c=at(.519,.055,a);box(stat,c[0],c[1],c[2],.008,.018,.018,0x858f8b)}
 }
 // Use the already validated four-wheel support plane, then the shared prop yaw.
 if(b.carPlane){const[floor,sx,sz]=b.carPlane;for(let i=meshStart;i<stat.length;i+=12){stat[i+1]+=floor-base+sx*(stat[i]-b.x)+sz*(stat[i+2]-b.z);const n=norm([stat[i+3]-sx*stat[i+4],stat[i+4],stat[i+5]-sz*stat[i+4]]);stat[i+3]=n[0];stat[i+4]=n[1];stat[i+5]=n[2]}}
}

function timberGate(g){
 const {x,z,width,angle,depth,inward,clearHeight}=g,base=groundAt(x,z),p=(u,y,d)=>facePoint(x,z,angle,u,base+y,d),r=width/2;
 // Full-depth brick returns bond into both street walls; the timber is set into
 // the rebate, with a small dark joint that reads as a real masonry connection.
 for(const side of[-1,1]){
  box(stat,...p(side*(r+.18),clearHeight/2,0),.36,clearHeight,depth,0xc2b297,angle,1);
  box(stat,...p(side*(r+.18),.12,0),.39,.24,depth+.06,0xa79b83,angle,7);
  box(stat,...p(side*(r+.09),clearHeight/2,inward*.14),.19,clearHeight,.28,0x514c41,angle,4);
  box(stat,...p(side*(r+.19),clearHeight/2,inward*.14),.025,clearHeight,.30,0x37362f,angle,5);
  box(stat,...p(side*(r+.18),clearHeight-.04,0),.43,.17,depth+.08,0xcabda4,angle,15);
  for(const f of[.16,.50,.84]){
   const y=clearHeight*f;
   // Three fixed wall straps meet the hinge barrels on each moving leaf.
   box(stat,...p(side*(r+.10),y,inward*.14),.30,.12,.055,0x3d403b,angle,5);
   for(const q of[-.08,.08])box(stat,...p(side*(r+.10)+q,y,inward*.18),.027,.027,.025,0xb2b0a3,angle,5);
  }
 }
 // A deep, restrained header has visible bearing at each end. No false arch or
 // suspended ornament remains beneath the recessed main vault.
 box(stat,...p(0,clearHeight+.19,0),width+.91,.38,depth+.12,0xb8aa91,angle,15);
 box(stat,...p(0,clearHeight+.018,inward*.14),width+.26,.14,.30,0x655b49,angle,4);
 box(stat,...p(0,clearHeight+.40,0),width+.98,.06,depth+.15,0xcbbda4,angle,7);
 const sill=(u,d)=>{const q=p(u,0,d);return[q[0],groundAt(q[0],q[2])+.018,q[2]]};
 const paving=[sill(-r,-depth/2),sill(r,-depth/2),sill(r,depth/2),sill(-r,depth/2)];quad(stat,...paving,0xffffff,2,paving.map(q=>[q[0]/3,q[2]/3]));
}
function doorHingeDetail(b){
 if(!b.hungGate)return;
 const base=groundAt(b.gate.x,b.gate.z),h=b.gate.clearHeight,side=b.hingeSide,u=side*b.w/2;
 // This routine emits leaf-local coordinates. The prop batching pass rotates
 // both leaf and hardware together around the same hinge-derived footprint.
 for(const f of[.16,.50,.84]){
  const y=base+h*f;
  box(stat,b.x+u,y,b.z,.10,.23,.12,0x3a3c37,0,5);
  for(const yy of[-.09,.09])box(stat,b.x+u,y+yy,b.z,.125,.025,.14,0x8d8d80,0,5);
  for(const face of[-1,1]){
   box(stat,b.x+u-side*.28,y,b.z+face*.105,.58,.10,.035,0x323630,0,5);
   for(const k of[.12,.29,.48])box(stat,b.x+u-side*k,y,b.z+face*.13,.026,.026,.02,0xbdb9a7,0,5);
  }
 }
}

function door(b){
 doorHingeDetail(b);
 const center=groundAt(b.x,b.z),cs=b.cs??Math.cos(b.rotation||0),sn=b.sn??Math.sin(b.rotation||0),floor=(u,d)=>groundAt(b.x+u*cs+d*sn,b.z-u*sn+d*cs),top=center+b.h;
 const p=(u,y,d)=>[b.x+u,y,b.z+d],n=Math.max(7,Math.ceil(b.w/.18));
 for(const side of[-1,1]){
  const depth=side*(b.d/2+.005),pt=(u,y,d)=>p(u,center+y,side*(b.d/2+d));
  for(let j=0;j<n;j++){const a=-b.w/2+j*b.w/n+.004,z=-b.w/2+(j+1)*b.w/n-.004;quad(stat,p(a,floor(a,depth)+.012,depth),p(z,floor(z,depth)+.012,depth),p(z,top,depth),p(a,top,depth),j%3?0x918978:0x766f62,4)}
  for(const u of[-b.w/2+.05,b.w/2-.05]){const lo=floor(u,depth);box(stat,b.x+u,(lo+top)/2,b.z+depth,.10,top-lo,.09,0x514b40,0,4)}
  for(const y of[.22,b.h*.48,b.h-.18]){box(stat,...pt(0,y,.055),b.w,.085,.055,0x353632,0,5);for(let k=-3;k<=3;k++)box(stat,...pt(k*b.w*.135,y,.09),.027,.027,.015,0xb8afa0)}
  for(const y of[b.h*.27,b.h*.72]){box(stat,...pt(0,y,.016),b.w*.75,b.h*.30,.03,0x7e7564,0,4);for(const u of[-1,1])box(stat,...pt(u*b.w*.38,y,.04),.05,b.h*.33,.04,0xa59880,0,4)}
  for(let k=0;k<12;k++){const a=k*TAU/12,z=(k+1)*TAU/12;beam(stat,pt(b.w*.30+Math.cos(a)*.09,b.h*.52+Math.sin(a)*.12,.13),pt(b.w*.30+Math.cos(z)*.09,b.h*.52+Math.sin(z)*.12,.13),.019,0x3c3b35,-1,4)}
 }
 // Solid end grain and a flush sill close the leaf to the local paving.
 for(const u of[-b.w/2,b.w/2])quad(stat,p(u,floor(u,-b.d/2),-b.d/2),p(u,floor(u,b.d/2),b.d/2),p(u,top,b.d/2),p(u,top,-b.d/2),0x7f7562,4);
 quad(stat,p(-b.w/2,top,-b.d/2),p(b.w/2,top,-b.d/2),p(b.w/2,top,b.d/2),p(-b.w/2,top,b.d/2),0x9f927c,4);
}

for(const b of cover){const start=stat.length;
 if(b.kind.startsWith('bridge')){bridgeProp(b)}else if(b.bridgeOpening){bridgeWall(b)}else if(b.kind==='vaultPier'){vaultPier(b)}else if(b.kind==='wall'){if(b.bWindow||b.bWindowFrame)continue;const bottom=b.foundation??Math.min(0,groundAt(b.x,b.z),...[-1,1].flatMap(u=>[-1,1].map(v=>groundAt(b.x+u*b.w/2*b.cs+v*b.d/2*b.sn,b.z-u*b.w/2*b.sn+v*b.d/2*b.cs))));box(stat,b.x,(bottom+b.top)/2,b.z,b.w,b.top-bottom,b.d,0xd4c3a2,0,1);box(stat,b.x,b.top+.045,b.z,b.w+.03,.09,b.d+.03,0xe0d2b5,0,7);if(b.architecturalClosure)closureDetail(b)}else if(b.kind==='parapet')parapet(b);else if(b.kind==='car')car(b);else if(b.kind==='door')door(b);else crate(b);
 if(b.rotation){const cs=Math.cos(b.rotation),sn=Math.sin(b.rotation);for(let i=start;i<stat.length;i+=VERTEX_SIZE){const x=stat[i]-b.x,z=stat[i+2]-b.z,nx=stat[i+3],nz=stat[i+5];stat[i]=b.x+x*cs+z*sn;stat[i+2]=b.z-x*sn+z*cs;stat[i+3]=nx*cs+nz*sn;stat[i+5]=-nx*sn+nz*cs}}
}
drawSiteLabels();
function arch(g){
 if(g.flatGate){timberGate(g);return}
 const {x:cx,z:cz,width,spring,angle,height:top,depth}=g,base=groundAt(cx,cz),r=width/2,stone=.32;
 const p=(u,y,d)=>facePoint(cx,cz,angle,u,base+y,d);
 for(const side of[-1,1]){
  box(stat,...p(side*(r+stone/2),top/2,0),stone,top,depth,0xd0c0a2,angle,1);
  box(stat,...p(side*(r+.16),.18,0),.40,.36,depth+.16,0xbbae95,angle,1);
  box(stat,...p(side*(r+.16),spring-.04,0),.43,.16,depth+.16,0xc5b599,angle,1);
 }
 // The shaped opening, soffit, spandrels and collision use exactly the same profile.
 for(let i=0;i<32;i++){
  const x0=-r+i*width/32,x1=-r+(i+1)*width/32,y0=gatewayRise(g,x0),y1=gatewayRise(g,x1);
  quad(stat,p(x0,y0,depth/2),p(x1,y1,depth/2),p(x1,top,depth/2),p(x0,top,depth/2),0xe0d3b8,0,[[x0/3,-y0/3],[x1/3,-y1/3],[x1/3,-top/3],[x0/3,-top/3]]);
  quad(stat,p(x1,y1,-depth/2),p(x0,y0,-depth/2),p(x0,top,-depth/2),p(x1,top,-depth/2),0xd0c1a4,0,[[x1/3,-y1/3],[x0/3,-y0/3],[x0/3,-top/3],[x1/3,-top/3]]);
  for(const side of[-1,1]){quad(stat,p(x1,y1,side*depth/2),p(x0,y0,side*depth/2),p(x0,y0+.045,side*(depth/2-.10)),p(x1,y1+.045,side*(depth/2-.10)),0xc6b69a,15);quad(stat,p(x1,y1+.045,side*(depth/2-.10)),p(x0,y0+.045,side*(depth/2-.10)),p(x0,y0+.045,0),p(x1,y1+.045,0),i%2?0xb5a488:0xc6b69a,15)}
 }
 const profile=archPoints(width,top,u=>gatewayRise(g,u),.17);
 for(const side of[-1,1]){const face=(u,v,d)=>p(u,v,side*(depth/2+d));brickRing(face,profile,.25,.065);for(const k of[-1,1])brickJamb(face,k,width,spring,.065)}
 // A simple fired-brick cap completes the street gateway.
 for(const [yy,hh,w,d,col]of[[top+.06,.16,width+.72,depth+.25,0xd1c3a9],[top-.11,.12,width+.58,depth+.14,0xb8aa8e]])box(stat,...p(0,yy,0),w,hh,d,col,angle,7);
 for(const side of[-1,1])for(const u of[-r-.17,r+.17]){
  box(stat,...p(u,spring*.5,side*(depth/2+.045)),.11,Math.max(.2,spring-.35),.08,0xf1f1f1,angle,7);
  for(let y=.45;y<spring-.15;y+=.38)box(stat,...p(u,y,side*(depth/2+.092)),.12,.018,.01,0x909090,angle);
 }
}

// B-courtyard wall matches the adjoining return in plan and its roof cornice.
function courtyardWindow(){
 const b=B_WINDOW;
 for(const q of B_WINDOW_PARTS){const top=q.fixedBottom===0&&q.fixedTop===b.sill?b.sill-.10:q.top;box(stat,q.x,(q.fixedBottom+top)/2,q.z,q.w,top-q.fixedBottom,q.d,0xd4c3a2,0,1);}
 box(stat,b.x,b.top+.04,(b.z0+b.z1)/2,b.thickness+.10,.12,b.z1-b.z0+.04,0xcabda5,0,7);
 box(stat,b.x,b.top-.12,(b.z0+b.z1)/2,b.thickness+.035,.10,b.z1-b.z0,0xa99b83,0,1);
 // Continuous metal sleeves project 120 mm from BOTH masonry faces.
 // The bottom rail's upper face is exactly the collision/landing height.
 const span=b.width-2*b.frameRail;
 for(const q of B_WINDOW_FRAME_PARTS)box(stat,q.x,(q.fixedBottom+q.top)/2,q.z,q.w,q.top-q.fixedBottom,q.d,0x42484c,0,-1);
 box(stat,b.x,b.sill-.155,b.z,b.thickness+.06,.11,b.width+.12,0xc6baa3,0,7);
 for(const side of[-1,1]){
  const angle=side*Math.PI/2;
  if(side===1)gerehPanel(b.x,b.head-.315,b.z,span-.10,.39,angle,0,false);
  // Mortared brick reveal frame, meeting the wall without a second wall skin.
  for(const z of[b.z-b.width/2-.11,b.z+b.width/2+.11])box(stat,b.x+side*(b.thickness/2+.018),(b.sill+b.head)/2,z,.036,b.head-b.sill+.32,.16,0xc2b197,0,15);
  for(const y of[b.sill-.10,b.head+.10])box(stat,b.x+side*(b.thickness/2+.018),y,b.z,.036,.16,b.width+.38,0xc2b197,0,15);
 }
}

}

function metricStaticSurfaces(){
 for(let i=0;i<stat.length;i+=36){
  const m=stat[i+11];if(![0,1,4,5,7,15].includes(m))continue;
  const normal=norm(cross([stat[i+12]-stat[i],stat[i+13]-stat[i+1],stat[i+14]-stat[i+2]],[stat[i+24]-stat[i],stat[i+25]-stat[i+1],stat[i+26]-stat[i+2]]));
  // Curved roof crowns already have arclength UVs; retain their continuous wrap.
  if(m===1&&Math.abs(normal[1])>.12&&Math.abs(normal[1])<.995&&ROOFS.some(r=>polygonContains(r.polygon,stat[i],stat[i+2])&&Math.abs(stat[i+1]-vaultHeightAt(r,stat[i],stat[i+2])-.31)<.04))continue;
  let U,V;if(Math.abs(normal[1])>.9999){U=[1,0,0];V=[0,0,1]}else{U=norm([normal[2],0,-normal[0]]);V=cross(normal,U);if(V[1]<0)V=V.map(n=>-n)}
  const scale=SURFACE_METERS[m];for(let j=0;j<36;j+=12){const x=stat[i+j],y=stat[i+j+1],z=stat[i+j+2];stat[i+j+9]=(x*U[0]+y*U[1]+z*U[2])/scale;stat[i+j+10]=(x*V[0]+y*V[1]+z*V[2])/scale}
 }
}
reconcileStaticSurfaces();
metricStaticSurfaces();
function compactStaticMesh(){let write=0;const seen=new Set();
 for(let i=0;i<stat.length;i+=36){const ax=stat[i+12]-stat[i],ay=stat[i+13]-stat[i+1],az=stat[i+14]-stat[i+2],bx=stat[i+24]-stat[i],by=stat[i+25]-stat[i+1],bz=stat[i+26]-stat[i+2];
  if(Math.hypot(ay*bz-az*by,az*bx-ax*bz,ax*by-ay*bx)<1e-8)continue;
  const key=[0,12,24].map(o=>stat.slice(i+o,i+o+3).map(v=>v.toFixed(6)).join(',')).sort().join('|')+';'+stat.slice(i+3,i+9).map(v=>v.toFixed(6)).join(',')+';'+stat[i+11];if(seen.has(key))continue;seen.add(key);
  for(let j=0;j<36;j++)stat[write++]=stat[i+j];
 }stat.length=write;
}
compactStaticMesh();
